We introduce CausalArena, a unified and evolvable benchmark for causal discovery under a common protocol. Synthetic SCMs supply controlled breadth over structures and mechanisms; semantic operational SCMs provide human-auditable, semantically grounded environments beyond standard synthetic generators; and formula-grounded SCMs test discovery under explicit scientific mechanisms. Public real-world datasets provide an additional external-validity check. Experiments across classical, neural, and pretrained methods reveal substantial ranking shifts across SCM families and protocols, showing that strong performance in one benchmark regime does not reliably transfer to others. These results highlight benchmark diversity and pretraining--evaluation overlap as central challenges for evaluating causal discovery in the foundation model era.

</details>


### [70] [TART: A Modular Tool for Technique-Aware Audio-to-Tablature Guitar Transcription](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11904)
*Akshaj Gupta,Hwi Joo Park,Andrea Guzman,Shamak Gowda,Samhita Konduri,Jiachen Lian,Robin Netzorg,Gopala Anumanchipalli*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出TART，一个模块化四阶段音频到吉他六线谱（tablature）流水线，首次实现从吉他音频直接生成含指法与演奏技法标注的六线谱，并在多个基准（含噪声场景）上取得领先结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动音乐转写（AMT）在吉他上受三方面限制：(1) 现有系统难以捕捉滑音、推弦、击弦等表现性技法；(2) 常将音符分配到错误的弦-品位组合；(3) 多在干净录音上训练，对真实噪声音频泛化差。

Method: TART为模块化四阶段音频到六线谱流水线：(1) 音频到MIDI转写模型；(2) 表现性技法分类器；(3) 以音频为条件的T5编码器-解码器用于弦-品位分配；(4) 自动化六线谱生成器。在GuitarSet、EGDB及两个增强基准（Noisy GuitarSet、Noisy EGDB）上以零样本（zero-shot）设置评估。

Result: 在四个基准上平均：audio-to-MIDI F50为81.35%（较最佳先前基线+6.67个点），弦-品位Tab F1为71.8%（+8.5个点），端到端Tab F1为54.08%。

Conclusion: 据作者所知，TART是首个直接从吉他音频生成同时包含指法与表现性技法标注的吉他六线谱框架。

Abstract: Automatic Music Transcription (AMT) for guitar remains limited by three challenges: existing systems often fail to capture expressive techniques such as slides, bends, and percussive hits; they often assign notes to incorrect string-fret combinations; and they are typically trained on clean recordings, limiting their generalization to noisy real-world audio. To address these challenges, we propose TART, a modular four-stage audio-to-tablature pipeline consisting of (1) an audio-to-MIDI transcription model, (2) an expressive technique classifier, (3) an audio-conditioned T5 encoder-decoder for string-fret assignment, and (4) an automated tablature generator. We evaluate TART in a zero-shot setting on GuitarSet, EGDB, and two augmented benchmarks, Noisy GuitarSet and Noisy EGDB. Averaged across these four benchmarks, TART achieves 81.35% audio-to-MIDI F50 (+6.67 points over the best prior baseline), 71.8% string-fret Tab F1 (+8.5 points over the best prior baseline), and 54.08% end-to-end Tab F1. To our knowledge, TART is the first framework to generate guitar tablature with both fingering and expressive technique annotations directly from guitar audio.

</details>


### [71] [From Protocols to Evidence: Bounded Claims for AI in Service of the Common Good](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11910)
*Nitesh V. Chawla,Paulo Benanti*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文主张AI不仅是治理问题，更是介入既有制度失败（响应性、归属感、关怀、问责）的手段；据此提出"断裂测试"以连接制度基线与系统评估，并区分"证据受限部署"与"测量受限治理"，在二者的边界内以RISE AI架构对责任、包容、安全、赋权作出有界证据主张。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有负责任AI研究多聚焦原则与合规协议（如欧盟AI法案、NIST AI RMF、ISO/IEC 42001），但未充分追问这些协议究竟确立了何种实质、保留了谁的权力、以及测量应在何处停止。作者认为AI部署本身即是对已失效制度的干预，可能修复、加剧、替代或掩盖这些失败，因此评估对象必须同时包括系统与制度断裂。

Method: 概念与框架构建：借助Pope Leo XIV《Magnifica Humanitas》以人的尊严、技术权力与共同善为核心的道德框架，提出"断裂测试"（rupture test），将制度基线与系统评估相衔接；并作概念区分——"证据受限部署"（将主张限于实际被评估的范围）与"测量受限治理"（记录有利证据也无法推翻的约束）。在此边界内引入RISE AI架构。

Result: 提出RISE AI架构，用于就责任（Responsibility）、包容（Inclusivity）、安全（Safety）、赋权（Empowerment）作出有界的、基于证据的主张；并给出"断裂测试"作为评估工具，明确制度断裂与系统评估之间的连接点及测量的止步之处。

Conclusion: 负责任AI不能仅靠更好的工程实现，还需制度修复以及持续不断的道德与政治判断；原则到协议的转化已在推进，但关键在于厘清协议的实际效力边界与权力盲区。

Abstract: Artificial Intelligence does more than create a governance problem. It can also reveal where institutions have already failed to provide responsiveness, belonging, care, and accountability. Once deployed, AI becomes an intervention in those conditions. It can repair, compound, substitute for, or conceal the failures it encounters. Responsible AI must therefore evaluate both the system and the institutional rupture into which it is introduced. The move from principles to protocols is already underway. The EU AI Act, NIST AI RMF, ISO/IEC 42001, and assurance practices translate commitments into roles, requirements, records, oversight, and assessment. The harder questions are what these protocols actually establish, whose power they leave untouched, and where measurement must stop. Pope Leo XIV's Magnifica Humanitas provides a broader moral frame centered on dignity, technological power, and the common good. Drawing on that frame, we develop a rupture test that links institutional baselines to system evaluation. We distinguish evidence-bounded deployment, which limits claims to what has actually been evaluated, from measurement-bounded governance, which records constraints that favorable evidence cannot override. Within those limits, RISE AI provides an architecture for making bounded, evidence-based claims about Responsibility, Inclusivity, Safety, and Empowerment. Responsible AI requires better engineering, institutional repair, and continued moral and political judgment.

</details>


### [72] [Data Scarcity and Model Sparsity: Mixtures-of-Experts Overfit More to Repeated Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11917)
*Atindra Jha,Margaret Li,Jure Leskovec,Percy Liang,Luke Zettlemoyer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究数据重复对稀疏架构（MoE）的影响，发现MoE比稠密模型更快退化，退化随稀疏度加剧且由总参数量决定；掩码正则化可缓解但无法完全匹配全唯一数据的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类写作文本供给耗尽，重复训练数据成为标准做法。已有工作研究了稠密Transformer的数据重复，但对近期主导、计算效率更高的稀疏架构（如MoE）在数据重复下的表现尚缺乏探索。

Method: 在单域与多域数据混合下改变数据重复率，并调整MoE设置（专家数量、粒度）；模型范围从80M到1B激活参数（8.5B总参数）；测试现有正则化方法（如dropout、遮蔽式正则化）作为补救；分析MoE过拟合的内部机制，包括路由稳定性与专家专业化。

Result: MoE在数据重复下退化更快，且随稀疏度增加而加剧，由总参数而非激活参数决定。80M稠密模型重复8倍以上仍退化极小，MoE在4倍即开始受损并迅速恶化，32倍后性能优势丧失、低于稠密模型。dropout等可缓解过拟合，强遮蔽正则化下MoE在重复超64倍时仍能胜过稠密模型，但无方法能完全匹配全唯一数据的性能。MoE路由在训练早期即稳定，专家专业化与对重复数据的过拟合相关。

Conclusion: 本文揭示了稀疏性与数据重复之间的不良交互，给出了过拟合核心机制及潜在补救措施的证据，并提出通过打破记忆模式来减少参数过度专业化是有前景的未来方向。

Abstract: As the supply of human-written text is exhausted, it has become standard practice to repeat language model training data. Prior work has studied data repetition for densely activated Transformers, but the effects of data repetition remains largely unexplored for recently dominant sparse architectures such as Mixture-of-Experts (MoE), despite their increased compute efficiency. We vary data repetition rates across single- and multi-domain data mixes, and across MoE settings, including expert count and granularity. We consistently find, for models ranging from 80M to 1B active (8.5B total) parameters, that MoEs degrade more rapidly under data repetition. This effect increases with sparsity, dictated by total rather than active parameters. While 80M dense models can repeat data over 8x with minimal degradation, MoEs instead begin to suffer at 4x, and deteriorate rapidly, ceding their performance benefits in all-unique data settings to underperform dense models after 32x. We experiment with existing regularization methods as a potential remedy. We find that some methods, such as dropout, can mitigate overfitting. In particular, with strong masking-based regularization, MoEs are able to outperform dense models even when data is repeated more than 64 times. However, no method fully matches the performance of all-unique training data. Finally, we analyze internal mechanisms correlated with MoE overfitting in high repetition regimes, and find that MoE routing universally stabilizes early in training, and that expert specialization correlates with overfitting to repeated data. In sum, our work addresses the adverse interactions between sparsity and data repetition: we present evidence for the core mechanisms of overfitting and its potential remediation, and suggest promising avenues for future methods to reduce over-specialization in model parameters by disrupting memorization patterns.

</details>


### [73] [General Quantification of Covariate and Concept Shifts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11918)
*Hongbo Chen,Li Charlie Xia*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文指出现有概念漂移定义在源域与目标域支撑不匹配时失效，基于熵最优传输提出 $\gamma^{*}$-概念漂移，推导统一协变量漂移与 $\gamma^{*}$-概念漂移的误差上界，并给出可估计算法与集中保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分布漂移下的泛化是现代机器学习的核心难题，但现有学习界理论局限于狭窄的理想化设定，且无法从样本中估计，导致理论与实际应用脱节。此外，现有概念漂移的定义在源域与目标域支撑不匹配时失效，缺乏通用且可估计的分析工具。

Method: 1) 揭示现有概念漂移定义在支撑不匹配时的失效问题；2) 借助熵最优传输（entropic optimal transport）提出 $\gamma^{*}$-概念漂移这一关键概念；3) 推导统一的误差上界，同时涵盖协变量漂移与 $\gamma^{*}$-概念漂移，适用于广泛的损失函数、标签空间及随机标注；4) 为这些漂移量设计具有集中保证的估计器，并提出 DataShifts 算法，用于量化分布漂移并估计误差上界。

Result: 提出的统一误差上界适用于广泛的损失函数、标签空间与随机标注；所设计的漂移估计器具备集中保证；DataShifts 算法可在多数应用场景中量化分布漂移并估计误差上界。

Conclusion: 本文弥合了分布漂移下学习误差理论与实际应用之间的鸿沟，提供了严谨且通用的分析工具，可用于在分布漂移下量化漂移并估计学习误差。

Abstract: Generalization under distribution shift remains a core challenge in modern machine learning, yet existing learning bound theory is limited to narrow, idealized settings and is non-estimable from samples. In this paper, we bridge the gap between theory and practical applications. We first show that existing definition of concept shift breaks when the source and target supports mismatch. Leveraging entropic optimal transport, we propose a key notion: $γ^{*}\!$-concept shifts, and derive a general error bound unifying covariate and $γ^{*}\!$-concept shifts, which applies to broad loss functions, label spaces, and stochastic labeling. We further develop estimators for these shifts with concentration guarantees, and the DataShifts algorithm, which can quantify distribution shifts and estimate the error bound in most applications - a rigorous and general tool for analyzing learning error under distribution shift.

</details>

### [74] [When Passing Tests Hides Vulnerabilities: An Empirical Study of Silent Failures in Agentic Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10548)
*Wenji Bai,Muhammad Waseem,Zeeshan Rasheed,Jaakko Peltonen,Pekka Abrahamsson*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该研究通过1030条有效执行轨迹（7个智能体框架、GPT-4o-mini、SecurityEval与CVEfixes数据集），经三轮定性编码与人工验证确认170例静默失败，划分为Omission（48.2%）、Introduction（30.6%）、Inadequacy（21.2%）三类共十种细粒度失效编码，揭示了通过测试却仍存在安全漏洞的修复失败模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于LLM的智能体自动代码修复受到广泛关注，但现有研究多聚焦于语法和功能验证是否通过，忽视了那些"通过验证却仍保留或引入安全漏洞"的补丁，即静默失败。此类失败更隐蔽、危害更大，因此需要系统识别并归类。

Method: 开展实证研究，收集7个智能体框架配合GPT-4o-mini在SecurityEval和CVEfixes两个安全数据集上产生的1030条有效执行轨迹；通过三轮迭代定性编码与人工验证，识别出170例确认的静默失败案例，并进行分类与编码分析。

Result: (i) 识别出三类主要静默失败：Omission占48.2%、Introduction占30.6%、Inadequacy占21.2%；(ii) 归纳出十种细粒度失效编码，刻画智能体遗漏安全控制、防御不完整或引入新漏洞的行为；(iii) 现有"通过测试"式评估与基于LLM的评审角色均无法暴露或拦截这些失败；(iv) 不同框架出现相似的不安全解决方案，暗示存在共享的模型、提示或任务层面影响，且单智能体与多智能体系统呈现不同的失败画像。

Conclusion: 该研究有助于研究者和实践者改进LLM智能体代码修复的评估方式，并推动开发超越功能正确性、覆盖所有生成产物的针对性验证方法。

Abstract: LLM-based agents for automated code repair have received significant attention in recent years from both research and software engineering practice perspectives. However, limited attention has been paid to patches that pass syntactic and functional verification but still retain or introduce security vulnerabilities. The aim of this research is to systematically identify and categorize such silent failures in LLM-based agentic code repair.
  We conducted an empirical study using 1,030 valid execution traces produced by seven agent frameworks with GPT-4o-mini across two security-focused datasets, SecurityEval and CVEfixes. Through three iterations of qualitative coding and manual verification, 170 confirmed silent failures were identified. The key results are: (i) Three main categories of silent failures were identified: Omission, Introduction, and Inadequacy. Omission accounts for 48.2% of the confirmed failures, Introduction for 30.6%, and Inadequacy for 21.2%. (ii) Ten fine-grained failure codes were classified under these three categories, showing how agents omit required security controls, apply incomplete defenses, or introduce new vulnerabilities during repair. (iii) Current test-passing evaluation and LLM-based reviewer roles were insufficient to expose or intercept these failures in the confirmed cases. (iv) Similar insecure solutions appeared across different frameworks, suggesting possible shared model-, prompt-, or task-level influences, while single-agent and multi-agent systems showed different failure profiles.
  The results of this study will assist researchers and practitioners in improving the evaluation of LLM-based agentic code repair and developing targeted verification methods that go beyond functional correctness and cover all generated artifacts.

</details>


### [75] [Optimizing AI Inference Across the Deployment Stack](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10550)
*Tejinder Singh,John Pflueger,Jeebak Mitra,Robert Lincourt,Mitchell Markow,Bhavesh A. Patel*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文对AI推理优化进行跨层统一分析，提出三层分类法、多目标优化形式化、部署排序泛函及证据协议，并综合边缘与数据中心证据表明部署结果由跨层交互决定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已发表基准测试常在不可比条件下报告延迟与吞吐，导致其难以支撑实际部署决策；而部署性能并非仅由模型架构决定，还受压缩、编译器变换与服务策略交互影响，因此需要统一的跨层分析框架与可比性证据规范。

Method: 提出三层分类法：模型级（量化、剪枝、蒸馏）、编译器变换（图融合、布局优化、内核自动调优）、系统策略（动态批处理、准入控制、内存分层）。将部署形式化为在精度、延迟、吞吐、内存占用与能耗上的约束多目标优化问题，分析具备帕累托单调性与尺度不变性的部署排序泛函；用Roofline模型刻画内存带宽层级对不同精度区的性能约束，用排队模型解释负载下服务时间变化对响应时间的放大。另提出证据协议：区分实测、推导与分析性声明，数值比较限制在同论文内，并要求报告硬件、软件版本、批语义与热状态。

Result: 综合了边缘平台（Jetson AGX Orin与五个推理框架）、数据中心GPU（A100、H100与三个LLM服务引擎）以及Llama-3.1系列量化研究。结果显示部署结果受跨层交互支配，任何单层分析均无法预测。

Conclusion: 给出约束感知的选择流程，并指出编译器—服务协同优化、跨硬件性能预测与标准化能耗报告等开放问题。

Abstract: AI deployment performance is shaped not by model architecture alone, but by interactions among compression, compiler transformations, and serving policies. Published benchmarks often report latency and throughput under incomparable conditions, limiting their use for deployment decisions. This paper presents a unified analytical treatment of inference optimization across the deployment stack. We introduce a three-layer taxonomy covering model-level techniques such as quantization, pruning, and distillation; compiler transformations such as graph fusion, layout optimization, and kernel autotuning; and system policies such as dynamic batching, admission control, and memory tiering. We formulate deployment as a constrained multi-objective optimization problem over accuracy, latency, throughput, memory footprint, and energy, and analyze a deployment-ranking functional with Pareto monotonicity and scale invariance. Roofline models show how memory-bandwidth hierarchies bound performance across precision regimes, while queuing models explain how service-time changes amplify response time under load. To improve comparability, we propose an evidence protocol that separates measured, derived, and analytical claims; limits numerical comparison to within-paper results; and requires reporting of hardware, software versions, batch semantics, and thermal state. We synthesize evidence from edge platforms, including Jetson AGX Orin and five inference frameworks; data center GPUs, including A100 and H100 with three LLM serving engines; and quantization studies across the Llama-3.1 family. The synthesis shows that deployment outcomes are governed by cross-layer interactions that no single-layer analysis can predict. We conclude with a constraint-aware selection procedure and open problems in compiler-serving co-optimization, cross-hardware performance prediction, and standardized energy reporting.

</details>


### [76] [ReqEvolve: User-Oriented Software Self-Evolution through Automatic Requirement Interpretation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10590)
*Md Asif Iqbal Fahim,Alessio Ferrari*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出 ReqEvolve——一个运行时用户驱动的软件自演化系统，通过融合需求工程与测试驱动开发，将高层用户请求转化为可执行功能，在 72 个演化案例上实现 89.2% Pass@1。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软件自演化系统虽能在运行时自主扩展功能，但演化请求通常源自终端用户，且很少以技术术语表达。开发者必须先将用户需求转译为技术规格，系统才能演化，这延迟了功能验证，用户无法立即观察所请求功能的行为。

Method: ReqEvolve 是一个运行时（runtime）代码生成系统，接受高层用户请求，集成自动需求工程（RE）与测试驱动开发（TDD），通过澄清（clarification）、规格分解（specification decomposition）、测试生成（test generation）和运行时集成（runtime integration）四个环节，将用户请求转化为可执行功能。

Result: 在 18 个项目、72 个软件演化案例上评估，对比基线为 SpecFix（面向 RE 的代码生成方法）与本文系统的消融变体。ReqEvolve 达到 89.2% Pass@1，比 SpecFix 高 18.8%（p < 0.01，r = 0.79，大效应量），比消融基线高 32.6%（p < 0.001，r = 0.88，大效应量）。

Conclusion: 结果为"用户驱动自演化"作为从用户请求自主扩展软件能力的可行范式提供了初步证据，可在开发者验证之前加速需求验证过程。

Abstract: The paradigm of software self-evolution enables systems to autonomously extend and reconfigure their own capabilities during execution in response to technical specifications. Yet requests for new functionality often originate from end users and are rarely expressed in technical terms. As a result, developers must translate user needs into technical specifications before the system can evolve, delaying early validation of the requested functionality by preventing users from immediately observing the resulting behaviour. To address this gap, we present ReqEvolve, a runtime code generation system that enables user-driven self-evolution by accepting high-level user requests. The system integrates automatic requirements engineering (RE) and test-driven development (TDD) to transform these requests into executable functionality through clarification, specification decomposition, test generation, and runtime integration. We evaluate ReqEvolve on 72 software evolution cases across 18 projects against two baselines: SpecFix, an RE-focused code generation approach, and an ablation variant of our system. ReqEvolve achieves 89.2% Pass@1, outperforming SpecFix by 18.8% (p < 0.01, r = 0.79, large effect) and the ablation baseline by 32.6% (p < 0.001, r = 0.88, large effect). These results provide initial evidence that user-driven self-evolution is a viable paradigm for autonomously extending software capabilities from user requests, thereby accelerating requirements validation prior to developer verification.

</details>


### [77] [Generative AI for trustworthy systems - Towards a health check model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10595)
*Jan Bosch,Rick Kazman,Henry Muccini,Helena Holmström Olsson*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出"可信自主性健康检查模型"（Trustworthy Autonomy Health Check Model），一个多维诊断工具，用于刻画组织在GenAI辅助软件工程中如何建立信任，替代传统单一维度的成熟度模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式AI在软件密集型系统中的采用迅速推进，但现有分析工具（主要是单维度成熟度模型）将重要的构型差异压缩到单一渐进轴上，无法捕捉组织在建立信任时多维度的配置性变化。

Method: 基于对来自电信、汽车、国防、航空、银行、能源、政府及企业软件服务领域十八位资深从业者的归纳式访谈研究，构建该模型。模型包含八个经验支撑维度，分为系统层（智能体权限范围、保障机制、数据可信度、架构约束、可追溯性与可理解性）与组织层（治理、人工监督姿态、劳动力能力可持续性），每维采用五级序数刻度；另有四类信任范式（操作型、工程型、统计型、基于约束型）作为交叉叠加层，刻画信任"如何"建立，与刻画"什么"需可信的维度互补。

Result: 模型为诊断性而非规范性：支持跨组织比较、揭示构型权衡，并在不施加强制单一演进路径的前提下定位组织所处位置。较高层级并非天然更优，目标是在各维度间实现与组织领域及所选信任范式相契合的一致性。该思想被形式化为"一致性假设"：有效可信度受制于智能体权限范围与支撑该范围的各维度之间的匹配程度，错配会导致病态风险或不必要的摩擦。

Conclusion: 论文讨论了从业者如何应用该模型，并概述了未来实证验证的方向。

Abstract: The adoption of generative AI in software-intensive systems is proceeding rapidly, but current analytical instruments - principally unidimensional maturity models - compress important configurational variation into a single progressive axis. Drawing on an inductive interview study of eighteen senior practitioners across telecommunications, automotive, defence, aviation, banking, energy, government, and enterprise software services, this paper presents the Trustworthy Autonomy Health Check Model: a structured, multidimensional instrument for characterizing how organizations establish trust in GenAI-assisted software engineering. The model organizes eight empirically grounded dimensions into a system layer (Scope of Agent Authority, Assurance Mechanisms, Data Trustworthiness, Architectural Containment, Traceability & Comprehensibility) and an organizational layer (Governance, Human Oversight Posture, Workforce Capability Sustainability), each on a five-level ordinal scale. A cross-cutting overlay of four trust paradigms - operational, engineering, statistical, and containment-based - captures how trust is established, complementing the dimensions capturing what must be trustworthy. The model is diagnostic rather than prescriptive: it supports cross-organizational comparison, surfaces configurational trade-offs, and locates an organization in a shared space without imposing a single progression path. Higher levels are not inherently better; the goal is alignment across dimensions appropriate to the organization's domain and chosen trust paradigm. This is formalized as the alignment hypothesis: effective trustworthiness is constrained by the fit between the scope of agent authority and the dimensions enabling it, with misalignment producing pathological risk or unnecessary friction. We discuss how practitioners can apply the model and outline directions for empirical validation.

</details>


### [78] [On the Relation between Code Quality and Machine Learning Performance: A Large-scale Empirical Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10610)
*Marius Mignard,Steven Costiou,Anne Etien*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该论文对 265,363 个 Kaggle Python notebook 进行大规模实证研究，揭示代码质量与机器学习性能的关系取决于所采用的质量维度，并检验流行度与作者专业度是否是质量或性能的有效代理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器学习社区普遍将模型性能视为首要指标，而将代码质量视为次要问题，这一优先级建立在一个基本未被检验的假设之上，即代码质量与 ML 性能互不相关。同时，实践者常复用通过社交信号（流行度、作者专业度）筛选出的 notebook 代码，而这些信号作为质量代理的可靠性从未被评估。因此有必要对上述假设与代理指标进行实证检验。

Method: 对提交至 Kaggle 竞赛的 265,363 个 Python notebook 开展大规模实证研究，使用两种静态分析工具评估代码质量：Pylint 用于衡量通用 Python 代码质量，以及配置了 34 条针对数据科学与 ML 实践的规则的 SonarQube，用于捕捉 ML 特定违规。

Result: 代码质量与性能的关系取决于所考虑的质量概念。通用 Python 代码质量与 ML 性能相解耦，在所有观测中均呈可忽略或不显著的关联；相反，ML 特定违规与性能之间呈现一致的小幅负相关，并在所有观测中稳定存在。notebook 的流行度无法提供关于代码质量或性能的信息。代码专业度对质量或性能均无信息量，而竞赛专业度与更好的性能、更少的 ML 特定违规相关，同时伴随略多的 Python 错误和重构违规。

Conclusion: 通用代码质量与 ML 性能解耦的假设在通用维度上成立，但 ML 特定实践违规确实与性能负相关，因此不能一概而论。流行度和作者专业度均不能作为代码质量或性能的可靠代理，实践者在复用 notebook 代码时应谨慎对待这些社交信号。

Abstract: Context: Computational notebooks are the standard environment for machine learning (ML) development. Within the ML community, model performance is often the primary considered metric, and code quality is treated as a secondary concern. This prioritization relies on a largely untested assumption that code quality and ML performance are unrelated. Practitioners also reuse existing code that may come from notebooks selected through social signals (popularity, author expertise) whose reliability as quality proxies has never been assessed. Objective: We empirically investigated the relationship between code quality and ML performance in notebooks, and evaluated whether popularity and author expertise give indication on code quality or performance. Method: We conducted a large-scale empirical study of 265,363 Python notebooks submitted to Kaggle competitions. We assessed code quality with two static analysis tools: Pylint, capturing general Python code quality, and SonarQube, configured with a profile of 34 rules targeting data-science and ML-specific practices. Results: The relationship between code quality and performance depends on the notion of quality considered. General Python code quality is decoupled from ML performance, showing negligible or non-significant correlations across all observations. In contrast, ML-specific violations exhibit a consistent, small negative association with performance that persists across all observations. The popularity of a notebook does not give information on the code quality or performance. Code expertise provides no information on quality or performance, but competition expertise correlates with better performance, fewer ML-specific violations, and slightly more Python errors and refactoring violations.

</details>


### [79] [AI Safety: Not Optional, Not Later](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10630)
*Qinghua Lu,Yoshua Bengio*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出一种"安全设计"（safety-by-design）保障架构，将模型层监督（如 Scientist AI）与系统层控制（脚手架与执行框架管控、独立验证、监控与证据基础设施）相结合，并以治理机制支撑问责与证据互操作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实事件表明，AI 安全失效往往并非发生在单一层面，而是跨多个层级共同出现。因此仅依靠模型层对齐或单一环节的防护不足以应对风险，需要从整体架构层面进行设计。

Method: 构建分层保障架构：（1）模型层监督，例如 Scientist AI；（2）系统层控制，涵盖对 scaffolds 与 harnesses 的管控；（3）独立验证；（4）持续监控；（5）证据基础设施；（6）以治理机制保障问责与证据互操作性，形成多层协同的设计方案。

Result: 摘要未给出实证实验或量化结果，仅为架构性提出，即以分层组合的方式将模型监督与系统控制、验证、监控、证据与治理整合为一体，用于应对跨层级的 AI 安全失效。

Conclusion: AI 安全保障应从设计阶段即采用多层级、端到端的架构思路，将模型级监督与系统级控制及治理基础设施结合，才能有效覆盖跨层失效路径并支撑问责与证据互通。

Abstract: Incidents show that AI safety failures often arise across multiple layers. We present a safety-by-design assurance architecture combining model-level supervision, such as Scientist AI, with system-level controls over scaffolds and harnesses, independent verification, monitoring, and evidence infrastructure, supported by governance for accountability and evidence interoperability.

</details>


### [80] [Numbat: Building and Verifying a Self-Contained Machine-Learning Stack](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10632)
*Thang Tran,Lan Dang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文报告了 numbat——一个用 Zig 单语言编写、无第三方运行时依赖的机器学习栈，并对其进行了五层级验证，以 25.9M 参数 YOLOv8m 类检测器在 COCO 2017 上完成 500 epoch 训练，导出权重经参考栈验证器评测得 0.4956 mAP50-95（官方端点 0.502），单 GPU 步时与参考实现持平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器学习系统几乎完全建立在少数大型 Python 编排框架之上，继承了这些技术栈的工程代价：数百个版本耦合包的运行环境、独立的部署导出工具链，以及研究语言与产品发布语言之间的分裂。作者希望用一个通用语言、无第三方运行时依赖的栈来消除这些成本。

Method: numbat 用单通用语言 Zig 实现，覆盖张量计算、自动微分、神经网络模块、混合精度、多 GPU 训练、数据加载与监控；其 SDK 以超过 1400 个入口点的稳定、增量版本化 C ABI 暴露，并提供六种语言绑定，临床领域平面把监管要求编码为可执行验收门而非文档。验证方面，把广泛使用的参考实现作为可执行规范，在五个层级上对其进行验证，从算子梯度检查到针对同机参考运行的自动化轨迹门，即“轨迹级差分预言机”。

Result: 验证协议发现十处静默配方分歧，并编目其机制与症状。作为验收测试，在 COCO 2017 上从随机初始化训练 25.9M 参数、YOLOv8m 级别的检测器，完整 500 epoch 调度，导出权重在官方协议下由参考栈自带验证器评测得 0.4956 mAP50-95（已发布端点 0.502），相同硬件上单 GPU 步时与之持平。权重、逐 epoch 指标与完整运行清单均已发布。

Conclusion: numbat 证明可以用单一通用语言构建无第三方运行时依赖、具备可验证性的完整机器学习栈，并通过轨迹级差分预言机等五层级验证捕捉到静默收敛退化；其检测器精度与单 GPU 步时均可与参考栈匹敌，相关权重、指标与运行清单已公开。

Abstract: Machine-learning systems are built almost exclusively on a few large Python-orchestrated frameworks, and they inherit those stacks' engineering costs: environments of hundreds of version-coupled packages, separate export toolchains for deployment, and the split between the language research is written in and the language products ship in. We report on the construction and verification of numbat, a machine-learning stack written in one general-purpose language (Zig) with no third-party runtime dependencies. The stack spans tensor computation, automatic differentiation, neural-network modules, mixed precision, multi-GPU training, data loading and monitoring; an SDK exposes it behind a stable, additively versioned C ABI of over 1,400 entry points, with bindings for six languages; and its clinical domain planes encode regulatory requirements as executable acceptance gates rather than documentation. Verifying such a stack is the harder half of building it: a defective training run rarely fails, it converges quietly to a slightly worse model. We treat a widely used reference implementation as an executable specification and verify against it at five levels, from operator gradient checks to an automated trajectory gate against a same-machine reference run - the arrangement our companion study formalizes as a trajectory-level differential oracle. The protocol surfaced ten silent recipe divergences, which we catalog with mechanisms and symptoms. As the acceptance test, we train a 25.9M-parameter detector of the YOLOv8m class from random initialization on COCO 2017 for the full 500-epoch schedule: the exported weights score 0.4956 mAP50-95 under the official protocol, scored by the reference stack's own validator (published endpoint 0.502), with single-GPU step time at parity on identical hardware. Weights, per-epoch metrics and the full run manifest are released.

</details>


### [81] [Governed Human-AI Prioritization Under Uncertainty: Adaptive Estimation and Dependency-Constrained Portfolio Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10648)
*Azzeddine Ihsine,Sara Ihsine*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文研究D-POAF决策实践中的五个量化算子（BVS、ERS、PVS、CCS、ODP），通过受控合成实验刻画其在已知潜变量与显式误差过程下的行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI原生的软件工程日益将人类判断、历史类比、参数估计与AI生成的预测融合于同一优先级决策中。核心问题不仅是候选工作的排序，更是如何在可审查、可重校准的前提下治理异质估计、不确定性、战略参数、依赖关系与有限容量。

Method: 对五个算子开展受控合成实验，考察其在不同潜变量与误差过程下的行为；分析BVS权重扰动、可靠性加权努力聚合、估计器漂移下的自适应可靠性加权、模型集体分歧检测，以及800个依赖约束组合优化实例中的表现，并以 bootstrap 区间验证。

Result: 中等BVS权重扰动保持全局排序（中位Spearman 0.986），更大战略变化使前10%重叠降至0.788；可靠性加权努力聚合MAE为0.616，较最佳单一估计器（MAE 1.073）降低42.6%，优于简单均值（0.638）；估计器漂移下自适应可靠性加权使平均RMSE较等权降低4.5%；模型集体分歧检测高误差优先级估计的ROC-AUC达0.906；在800个依赖约束组合实例中，价值/努力的均值目标比0.962、ODP距离0.953（相对精确最优）。

Conclusion: 结果为证据加权的人机协同优先级排序建立了量化基础，并界定了最短路径ODP形式与一般发布组合选择之间的优化边界。

Abstract: AI-native software engineering increasingly combines human judgment, historical analogy, parametric estimation, and AI-generated forecasts inside the same prioritization decision. The resulting problem is not merely how to rank candidate work, but how to govern heterogeneous estimates, uncertainty, strategic parameters, dependencies, and limited capacity in a way that remains inspectable and recalibratable. We study five quantitative operators used in the D-POAF decision practice: Business Value Score (BVS), Effort and Risk Score (ERS), Prioritization Value Score (PVS), Collective Calibration Score (CCS), and Optimal Development Path (ODP). Controlled synthetic experiments characterize their behavior under known latent variables and explicit error processes. Moderate BVS-weight perturbations preserved global rankings (median Spearman 0.986), while broader strategic changes reduced top-10% overlap to 0.788. Reliability-weighted effort aggregation achieved MAE 0.616, a 42.6% reduction relative to the best individual estimator (MAE 1.073), and outperformed a simple mean (MAE 0.638). Under estimator drift, adaptive reliability weighting reduced average RMSE by 4.5% relative to equal weighting. Model-collective divergence detected the highest-error priority estimates with ROC-AUC 0.906. In 800 dependency-constrained portfolio instances, value-to-effort achieved mean objective ratio 0.962 and ODP distance 0.953 against the exact optimum; bootstrap intervals confirm a small but systematic advantage for value-to-effort under the declared objective. These results establish a quantitative basis for evidence-weighted human-AI prioritization and define the optimization boundary between shortest-path ODP formulations and general release-portfolio selection.

</details>


### [82] [LLMVul: A Vulnerability-Labeled Dataset of LLM-Generated C/C++ Functions from Real Production Repositories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10945)
*Mohammad Farhad,Shuvalaxmi Dass*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该论文提出LLMVul数据集，从真实生产仓库中挖掘LLM生成的C/C++函数并标注漏洞，包含21,430个函数、1,540个漏洞函数、17类CWE，标注一致性Cohen's kappa为0.79。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有漏洞数据集多聚焦人工编写代码或受控提示环境，难以研究真实软件项目中LLM生成代码的安全弱点，缺乏真实世界LLM生成代码的漏洞标注数据。

Method: 从GitHub上4年（2022-11-13至2026-09-03）挖掘AI辅助开发活动，利用提交元数据、AI相关作者证据等来源信号识别LLM生成代码；经筛选与去重获得21,430个C/C++函数（来自226个仓库）；采用静态分析与模式匹配互补技术集成标注漏洞，并为确认漏洞函数分配CWE类别；另进行独立人工标注并以Cohen's kappa衡量一致性。

Result: 数据集含21,430个唯一C/C++函数，覆盖226个仓库；其中1,540个集成判定为漏洞函数，涵盖17个CWE类别；人工标注一致性Cohen's kappa为0.79；漏洞函数数量显著多于现有面向漏洞的LLM代码基准。

Conclusion: LLMVul同时保留代码级漏洞标签与生成/溯源元数据，支持漏洞检测、LLM生成代码安全评估及AI辅助软件开发中漏洞模式的可复现研究，数据集已公开。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used to generate and assist with software development, yet existing vulnerability datasets largely focus on human-written code or controlled prompting environments. This limits the ability to study security weaknesses in LLM-generated code as it appears in real-world software projects. We present LLMVul, a vulnerability-labeled dataset of LLM-generated C/C++ functions mined from real production repositories. We mine AI-assisted development activity from GitHub over a 4 year period, from November 13, 2022 to September 3, 2026, using provenance signals such as commit metadata and AI-related authorship evidence. After filtering and deduplication, LLMVul contains 21,430 unique C/C++ functions from 226 repositories, together with repository, commit, function, provenance, and AI-tool metadata. We establish vulnerability labels using an ensemble of complementary static-analysis and pattern-based techniques and assign Common Weakness Enumeration (CWE) categories to confirmed vulnerable functions. To assess labeling reliability, we additionally conduct independent manual annotation and measure inter-rater agreement using Cohen's kappa ($k=0.79$). LLMVul contains 1,540 ensemble-vulnerable functions spanning 17 unique CWE categories, providing substantially more real-world LLM-generated vulnerable C/C++ functions than existing vulnerability-oriented LLM code benchmarks. By preserving both code-level vulnerability labels and generation/provenance metadata, LLMVul enables reproducible research on vulnerability detection, security evaluation of LLM-generated code, and analysis of vulnerability patterns in AI-assisted software development. The LLMVul dataset is publicly available at https://doi.org/10.5281/zenodo.22668216.

</details>


### [83] [What a Random Draw from the MCP Registry Contains, and What Tool-Use Benchmarks Contain Instead](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10962)
*Haseeb Mohammed Afsar*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 基于对 MCP 服务器生态的未修复概率抽样（400 个 npm/stdio 服务器线级探测），作者发现仅 48.8% 能完成 initialize 握手，主要失败原因是服务器根本无法启动（37.5%）而非缺少凭证（13.3%）；运行中的服务器 JSON Schema 硬合规性完美，但可选安全注释缺失率高达 58.8%。工具描述比对显示真实 MCP 工具近重复率仅 2.8% 且均为服务器内部重复，跨作者重复为 0.0%；而 BFCL v4 近重复率达 16.7%、UltraTool 为 0.3%，且原始数据行大量为名+描述完全重复（BFCL 68.8%、UltraTool 85.6% vs 真实 MCP 0.4%），意味着未做全局去重的基准统计实际测量的是重复率而非工具本身。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 MCP 服务器生态研究普遍采用参考集、热门榜单、人工筛选框架或"修复至可用"的流水线来抽取样本，这类抽样方式暗中筛选出了"能正常工作"的服务器，导致对生态现状的估计存在系统性偏差，无法反映真实分布。作者旨在通过未修复的概率抽样揭示生态的真实面貌。

Method: 从 24,135 个服务器的注册表普查中，以公开种子抽取 400 个 npm/stdio 服务器，逐个进行线级（over the wire）探测；同时用同一测量工具对照一个人工筛选框架。随后将真实 MCP 工具描述与 BFCL v4、UltraTool 两个工具使用基准语料，在固定单一方法下进行近重复检测（余弦相似度阈值 0.70 等）。所有数据可由公开脚本与种子复现。

Result: 未修复样本中仅 48.8% 完成 initialize 握手，人工筛选框架为 66.7%；主要失败为服务器无法启动（37.5%）而非缺凭证（13.3%）。195 个可运行服务器上硬合规性完美：2,766 个广告工具零致命 JSON Schema 违规。可选安全注释缺失率随机抽样为 58.8%，筛选框架为 41.5%。真实 MCP 工具近重复率 2.8% 且全为服务器内部，跨作者重复在所有阈值下均为 0.0%；BFCL v4 为 16.7%（其中 16.4 个百分点位于独立呈现的任务之间），UltraTool 仅 0.3%。原始数据中名+描述完全重复率：BFCL 68.8%、UltraTool 85.6%，真实 MCP 仅 0.4%。

Conclusion: MCP 生态研究中普遍存在的抽样偏差（倾向选取可用服务器）会显著美化握手成功率与安全注释覆盖等指标；基准语料的统计在未做全局去重时实际测量的是重复而非工具多样性，且 BFCL 的高近重复是其自身特性而非合成语料类的普遍问题。作者呼吁采用未修复的概率抽样与全局去重方法来获得真实生态度量。

Abstract: Studies of the Model Context Protocol (MCP) server ecosystem draw their samples in ways that quietly select for servers that work: reference sets, popularity lists, hand-curated frames, or pipelines that repair a server until it starts. We report what an unrepaired probability sample actually contains. From a 24,135-server registry census we draw 400 npm/stdio servers with a published seed and probe each one over the wire. Only 48.8% complete an initialize handshake, against 66.7% for a hand-curated frame measured with the same instrument, and the dominant failure is not missing credentials (13.3%) but servers that never start at all (37.5%). Among the 195 that do run, hard conformance is total: zero fatal JSON Schema violations across 2,766 advertised tools. Optional safety annotations are the real variance, and the tool-level omission rate on a random draw is 58.8% against 41.5% on the curated frame, so curation flatters this figure too. We then compare the tool descriptions these servers advertise against two tool-use benchmark corpora using one method held constant. Real MCP tools show 2.8% near-duplication at cosine 0.70, and all of it lies within single servers: cross-author near-duplication is 0.0% at every threshold tested. BFCL v4 shows 16.7%, of which 16.4 points lie between independently presented tasks. UltraTool shows 0.3%, cleaner than real tools, so this is a property of BFCL and not of synthetic corpora as a class. Separately, 68.8% of raw BFCL rows and 85.6% of raw UltraTool rows are exact name-plus-description repeats, against 0.4% for real MCP, so any statistic computed over these releases without global deduplication measures repetition rather than tools. All figures regenerate from released scripts and a published seed.

</details>


### [84] [Engineering Reliable Commit Gates for Agentic AI: Cost-Aware Verification Portfolios under Common-Mode Data Failures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10969)
*Zihao Zheng,Baichuan Li,Junyi Yao,Jiayu Long*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出 VP-CONTROL——一个面向智能体状态变更动作的成本感知提交门控运行时保障设计与确定性基准，通过 48 个任务模板生成 2,880 个场景，揭示证据来源多样性远重于验证器模型多样性，并强调提交时刻强制执行的必要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能体系统会执行改变状态的动作，而附加的验证器可能继承同一上游故障，导致不安全提交。现有实践往往混淆"验证器模型多样性"与"证据来源多样性"的贡献，且缺乏成本感知、可复现的评估基准与运行时保障机制。

Method: 设计 VP-CONTROL 运行时保障架构与确定性基准：48 个任务模板 × 六类故障模式 = 2,880 场景；固定调用次数的 2×2 实验，分离验证器模型多样性与证据来源多样性；基于仅可观测部署元数据的组合控制器选择验证方案；采用近似聚类调整校准；并开展预注册的实时 HTTP/SQLite 研究，测试并发写入与响应丢失。

Result: 在冻结提案上，共享证据的跨模型投票批准了 62.9% 的不安全提案，而独立证据来源仅为 22.9%；来源效应达 40.9 个百分点，模型多样性效应为 11.3 个百分点。组合控制器在名义 5% 目标下实现 1.9% 不安全执行与 38.2% 自动化安全覆盖率；匹配预算组合优于固定验证策略。迁移具有条件性：未见故障族风险 16–26%，FinQA 检验未能复现来源效应。实时研究中，检查后竞态击败纯验证器门控；事务性部分防护仅阻止已覆盖故障，完整原子防护在 216 个回合中未记录不安全效果；幂等请求标识防止响应丢失后的重复效果。

Conclusion: 结果支持显式证据血缘、成本感知选择与提交时刻强制执行三项设计原则，同时暴露近似校准与本地工具泛化的局限。

Abstract: Agentic systems commit state-changing actions, but additional verifiers can inherit the same upstream fault. We present VP-CONTROL, a runtime-assurance design and deterministic benchmark for cost-aware commit gates. Its 48 task templates yield 2,880 scenarios across six fault regimes. A fixed-call 2 x 2 experiment separates verifier-model diversity from evidence-source diversity. On frozen proposals from two local actor families, a cross-model vote over shared evidence approves 62.9% of unsafe proposals, versus 22.9% with an independent source. The source effect is 40.9 percentage points, compared with 11.3 for model diversity. A portfolio controller selects verification plans using only deployment-observable metadata. Approximate cluster-adjusted calibration at a nominal 5% per-task target yields 1.9% unsafe execution and 38.2% automated safe coverage on the locked test. Matched-budget portfolios also improve on fixed verification policies. Transfer remains conditional: unseen fault families yield 16-26% risk, and a FinQA check fails to reproduce the source effect with the tested small verifiers. A preregistered live HTTP/SQLite study tests concurrent writes and lost responses. After-check races defeat verifier-only gates; transactional partial guards prevent only covered failures, while a full atomic guard records no unsafe effects across 216 episodes. Idempotent request identifiers prevent duplicate effects after lost responses. The results motivate explicit evidence lineage, cost-aware selection, and commit-time enforcement, while exposing the limits of approximate calibration and local-tool generalization.

</details>


### [85] [RCL: A Retrieval-Confidence Layer for Detecting Insufficient Context in Enterprise Retrieval-Augmented Code Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11023)
*Chandra Mohan Ravuri*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出RCL（检索置信层），在检索与生成之间插入轻量模块，用调用图结构覆盖分与新颖性分联合判断私有代码库检索是否充分，不足时触发追加检索或转人工审核。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 企业私有代码库中的私有API、内部框架与未文档化约定处于模型预训练分布之外，检索缺陷无法被参数化知识补偿。已有研究显示即使oracle检索也无法消除错误、失败在下游API使用；开放域问答中的置信度门控检索依赖模型内部置信度。二者都未在生成前判断检索本身对私有代码查询是否结构性充分。

Method: RCL插入检索与生成之间，由两部分评分构成：基于调用图推导的结构覆盖分，以及估计查询对模型先验之外知识依赖程度的新颖性分；两者结合检测检索不足。置信度低于校准阈值时，触发定向追加检索，或标记输出供人工审核，而非在上下文不完整时静默生成。论文给出架构描述、评分函数形式化，并提出评测方法：向开源Java仓库注入合成内部API构建私有代码基准，以模拟企业条件而不使用专有代码。

Result: 第7节报告了RCL与仅基于相似度的检索在生成正确率上的对比结果。

Conclusion: 论文主张：检索充分性应基于结构而非模型内部置信度来评估，这是在私有企业场景下构建更安全代码生成系统的一个独立且目前被忽视的信号。

Abstract: Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) for code generation has been studied extensively on public repositories, where a model's parametric knowledge often compensates for imperfect retrieval. This breaks down in enterprise codebases, where private APIs, internal frameworks, and undocumented team conventions fall entirely outside any model's pretraining distribution. Recent work on private-library code generation shows that even oracle (perfect) retrieval does not eliminate errors, but locates failures downstream in API usage; separately, confidence-gated retrieval has been studied for open-domain question answering using model-internal confidence. Neither addresses whether retrieval itself was structurally sufficient for a private-code query before generation begins. We introduce RCL (Retrieval-Confidence Layer), a lightweight module inserted between retrieval and generation that combines a call-graph-derived structural coverage score with a novelty score estimating a query's dependence on knowledge outside the model's prior, to detect insufficient retrieval before generation occurs. When confidence falls below a calibrated threshold, RCL triggers a targeted follow-up retrieval or labels the output for human review, rather than generating silently against incomplete context. We describe RCL's architecture, formalize its scoring functions, and propose an evaluation methodology using a private-code benchmark built by injecting synthetic internal APIs into open-source Java repositories, simulating the enterprise condition without proprietary code. We report results (Section 7) comparing RCL against similarity-only retrieval on generation correctness. Our position is that retrieval sufficiency, assessed structurally rather than from model-internal confidence, is a distinct and currently underaddressed signal for building safer code-generation systems in private, enterprise settings.

</details>


### [86] [SaltBench: A Referee-Gated Protocol for Measuring Method Effects in Machine-Checked Software Work](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11076)
*Jason Hickey*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出 SaltBench 基准协议，用于测量"机器裁判"（证明内核、程序验证器、隐藏测试集）如何改变编码智能体的行为方式，并报告在 5 个 Rust/Verus 组件上的初步实验结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器裁判掌握对智能体工作价值的最终判定权，且智能体无法申辩，因此需要一套可测量其效应、且结论无法事后叙述性修饰的实验协议。

Method: 以"座位"（标准工具链中的智能体会话）为被试；所有结果在智能体自有工具链之外裁定，通过探针测试网络/参考解/评测框架的隔离墙，隔离为观测而非假设；每次运行由带日期的冻结授权并预先登记预测；预算中止视为停机而非失败。5 个组件均以 Rust 在固定版本 Verus 工具链下编写，裁判为隐藏测试集。设 4 个实验臂：纯智能体；被指示编写规约并据此验证代码的智能体（方法的简化呈现）；以及两个先验给定规约的臂，经带日期修订扩展至 k=4。

Result: 登记符号检验未达裁决（3/4，p=0.3125，所有溢价低于可分辨下限）。被指示"编写规约并验证"的臂在全部 5 个组件上成本更高，且差距具实践意义：在两种声明集解读下溢价均不超过 2.8879×，最便宜的三个组件低于 1.4×。该上界仅属此样本总体：溢价在最小组件上接近 1，并随规模增大而上升。

Conclusion: SaltBench 提供了使裁判效应可测量、结果不可事后叙述的协议，并公布完整记录；"规约+验证"指令会带来随组件规模增长的成本溢价，但本样本的溢价上界不构成对更大总体的承诺。

Abstract: SaltBench is a benchmark protocol for one question: How does a machine referee change the way a coding agent works? A machine referee --- a proof kernel, a program verifier, or a withheld test suite --- decides what an agent's work is worth, and the agent cannot argue with it. Here we report a protocol that makes the referee's effect measurable and whose answers cannot be narrated afterwards: every outcome is decided outside the agent's own toolchain; the agent is walled off from the network, the reference solutions and the harness itself, and the wall is tested by probes that try to breach it before any scored run, so the isolation is observed rather than assumed; every run is authorized by a dated freeze with its predictions registered; and a budget stop is a halt, never a failure. In this study, the subject of the benchmark is a ``seat'', meaning an agent session in its standard harness. We tested five systems components, all authored in Rust under a pinned Verus toolchain, with a withheld test suite as the referee for each. Four arms are tested: a plain agent; an agent that is also instructed to create a specification and verify the code against it, in a reduced rendering of the method, as registered; and two arms where the specification is provided a priori, extended under a dated amendment to $k=4$, where the registered sign test reached no verdict (3 of 4, $p = 0.3125$, every premium below the resolvable floor). We found that the arm instructed to specify and verify cost more on all five components, and by a practical margin: across these five components no premium exceeded $2.8879\times$ under either reading of the declared set, and the three cheapest sat below $1.4\times$. That bound is a property of this population and not a promise about larger ones: the premium runs near $1$ on the smallest components and rises with size. We publish the complete record.

</details>


### [87] [A Model-Centric DevOps Architecture for DEVS-Based Digital Twin Simulation Services](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11122)
*Arnis Lektauers,Gusts Linkevičs,Guntis Mosāns,Arina Fokina,Rasa Gulbe*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该文提出面向DEVS数字孪生的模型中心DevOps架构，将仿真模型作为一等制品，用声明式YAML（映射到multiPDEVS）在CI/CD中校验并生成不可变版本化制品，并以Kubernetes容器微服务承载，含状态外化与生命周期控制的引擎改造；以里加22路公交走廊为案例验证全生命周期，单容器引擎在约47,870个原子组件场景下报告吞吐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数字孪生仿真模型像软件一样演进与重部署，但基于DEVS的引擎虽具形式化基础，却缺乏版本管理、自动化校验与云原生持续交付支持，导致模型生命周期管理在多数部署中处于临时、非规范状态。

Method: 提出模型中心的DevOps架构：将仿真模型定义为一等DevOps制品，采用声明式YAML语言并给出到multiPDEVS的形式化映射；在CI/CD流水线中执行结构与语义校验，产出不可变、带版本的制品（回退即固定版本标识）；平台以Kubernetes上的容器化微服务分解，并对引擎进行状态外化与生命周期控制适配。

Result: 以里加Route 22公共交通走廊为初始案例（里加全市多模态交通数字孪生的首次实例化），完整跑通了生命周期，并报告了约47,870个DEVS原子组件场景下的单容器引擎吞吐；流水线级捕获统计与集群级并发多场景执行由配套实证研究给出。

Conclusion: 该工作将DEVS数字孪生仿真服务化部署纳入规范化DevOps流程，实现可校验、可版本化、可回退的模型生命周期管理；当前结果限于案例与单容器性能，流水线与集群扩展性有待配套研究验证。

Abstract: Digital twin simulation models are evolved and redeployed like software, yet DEVS-based engines offer a sound formal basis with little support for versioning, automated validation, or continuous delivery in cloud-native environments, leaving model lifecycle management ad hoc in most deployments. This paper proposes a model-centric DevOps architecture for deploying DEVS-based digital twin simulations as managed services. Simulation models are treated as first-class DevOps artefacts defined in a declarative YAML language with a formal mapping to multiPDEVS, supporting structural and semantic validation in a CI/CD pipeline that produces immutable versioned artefacts, so that reverting to an earlier validated version reduces to pinning its identifier. The platform is decomposed into containerised microservices on Kubernetes, with engine adaptations for state externalisation and lifecycle control. An initial case study on the Riga Route 22 public-transit corridor, the first instantiation of a planned city-wide multi-modal transport digital twin for Riga, Latvia, exercises the full lifecycle and reports single-container engine throughput for a scenario with roughly 47,870 DEVS atomic components; pipeline-level catch statistics and cluster-level concurrent multi-scenario execution are the subject of companion empirical studies.

</details>


### [88] [FST Pay: Deterministic Safety-Gated Architecture for Youth Digital Payments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11195)
*Shaikh Mohammed Burhan,Syed Farhaan Quadri,Tabassum Nahid Sultana*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: FST Pay 提出一种面向青少年实时支付的架构与形式化规范：在授权路径上采用严格确定性安全门控（六项不变量检查，输出 ALLOW/REVIEW/BLOCK），将生成式AI完全下移至结算后的解释层，从而在不牺牲可审计性的前提下防控青少年支付风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数字支付基础设施使青少年可直接接入实时金融服务。早期接入虽有助于金融素养与数字普惠，但也带来冲动消费、社会工程诈骗、未授权交易与商户剥削等风险。传统对策依赖概率型机器学习或僵化的静态控制；而让概率型或生成式AI模型直接影响实时支付授权，会引入非确定性、不可预测的边界行为与关键审计漏洞。因此需要一个既能实时防护又保持确定性与可审计性的架构。

Method: FST Pay 以不可变操作边界为核心：实时授权路径上执行严格确定性安全门控，下游AI解释与决策解耦。对经UPI等通道发起的交易执行六项确定性不变量检查——支出限额、监护人共签策略、交易金额阈值、商户类别码（MCC）、时间访问区间、硬件完整性约束。通过有序且互斥的决策函数将交易严格分类为ALLOW、REVIEW或BLOCK。高风险交易触发异步监护人共签工作流。生成式AI被完全置于结算之后，仅消费已发布的事后决策事件以生成自然语言财务洞察，不拥有对账本的变更权限。

Result: 该文给出了FST Pay的架构设计与形式化规范，明确了六项不变量检查、三值互斥决策函数、异步监护人共签机制以及下游AI解释的事件驱动接口。核心结果是建立了“确定性授权 + 解耦式AI解释”的可审计边界，消除了概率型/生成式AI对实时授权与账本状态的直接干预。

Conclusion: 在青少年实时支付场景中，安全性应由授权路径上的确定性不变量与有序互斥决策函数保障，而生成式AI的价值应限定在结算后的事件驱动解释与财务洞察生成。该架构在实现金融普惠与素养提升的同时，避免了非确定性与审计漏洞，为受监管环境下的青少年支付系统提供了可形式化验证的设计范式。

Abstract: Digital payment infrastructures increasingly provide adolescent users with direct access to real-time financial services. While early access promotes financial literacy and digital inclusion, it exposes young users to severe risks of impulsive spending, social engineering frauds, unauthorized transactions, and merchant exploitation. Conventional countermeasures rely on probabilistic machine learning or rigid static controls. However, allowing probabilistic or generative artificial intelligence (AI) models to directly influence real-time payment authorization introduces non-determinism, unpredictable edge-case behavior, and critical audit vulnerabilities. This paper introduces Financial Safety for Teens Pay (FST Pay) as an architectural and formal specification. FST Pay is founded on an immutable operational boundary: strict deterministic safety gating on the real-time authorization path coupled with decoupled downstream AI explanation. Transactions initiated via rails like UPI are subjected to six deterministic invariant checks covering spending limits, guardian co-sign policies, transaction amount thresholds, merchant category codes, temporal access intervals, and hardware integrity constraints. Transactions are classified strictly into ALLOW, REVIEW, or BLOCK outcomes through an ordered, mutually exclusive decision function. High-risk transactions trigger an asynchronous guardian co-sign workflow. Generative AI is relegated entirely downstream of settlement, consuming published post-decision events solely to generate natural-language financial insights without holding mutation privileges over the ledger.

</details>


### [89] [TripleBound: Triplet-Guided Heterogeneous Graph Learning for Microservice Decomposition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11212)
*Mineth Weerasinghe,Himindu Kularathne,Methmini Madhushika,Danuka Lakshan,Nisansa de Silva,Adeesha Wijayasiri,Srinath Perera*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出 TripleBound，一种结合异构图神经网络与弱监督三元组约束的单体到微服务自动化分解框架，在多数数据集上取得最优综合分解得分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单体到微服务迁移因紧耦合与边界不清而困难；现有分解方法多仅依赖结构依赖或语义相似性单一信号，很少在统一表示学习目标中融合两者。

Method: 在异构图神经网络上增强弱监督三元组约束：结构依赖由 CHGNN 建模（程序节点、资源节点、CALL 边、CRUD 边构成异构图）；语义关系通过解析器推断的服务分组（基于包结构、命名约定、代码位置）生成三元组约束，并注入共享结构隐空间，实现两类信号联合优化。

Result: 在 AcmeAir、DayTrader、PlantsByWebSphere、JPetStore 上评估：在所选权重下，TripleBound 在 AcmeAir、DayTrader、JPetStore 综合分解得分最高（优于 CHGNN 与 MonoEmbed），而 CHGNN 在 PlantsByWebSphere 更强。逐指标分析显示结构模块度与分区间耦合提升，但部分数据集实体分布不均衡度增高；替换综合权重后 TripleBound 在 AcmeAir、DayTrader 保持第一，JPetStore 则不然。

Conclusion: 融合结构与语义信号的统一表示学习可提升单体分解质量，但综合排名依赖指标权重，存在模块度与实体分布均衡性之间的权衡。

Abstract: Cloud computing and DevOps have made microservices a common architecture for scalable, maintainable software systems. However, migrating monoliths to microservices remains challenging due to tight coupling and unclear service boundaries. Existing decomposition approaches typically rely on either structural dependencies or semantic similarity signals, but rarely integrate both within a unified representation learning objective. This paper proposes TripleBound, a hybrid framework for automated monolith-to-microservices decomposition that augments a heterogeneous graph neural network with weakly supervised triplet constraints derived from parser-inferred service groups based on package structure, naming conventions, and code location. TripleBound injects triplet-based constraints directly into the shared structural latent space, enabling both signals to be jointly optimized during representation learning. Structural dependencies are captured using CHGNN, which models the monolith as a heterogeneous graph with program nodes, resource nodes, CALL edges, and CRUD edges. Semantic relationships are incorporated through triplet constraints generated from parser-inferred service groups. Evaluation on AcmeAir, DayTrader, PlantsByWebSphere, and JPetStore shows that TripleBound achieves the highest composite decomposition score under the selected weighting on AcmeAir, DayTrader, and JPetStore compared to CHGNN and MonoEmbed, while CHGNN remains stronger on PlantsByWebSphere. Per-metric analysis reveals trade-offs: gains in structural modularity and inter-partition coupling are accompanied by higher entity distribution imbalance on some datasets. Alternative composite weightings preserve TripleBound's first-place ranking on AcmeAir and DayTrader but not on JPetStore, showing that the aggregate ranking is metric-dependent.

</details>


### [90] [Exploring the Role of Security Experience and ChatGPT Usage Strategies on Secure Software Engineering Education](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11303)
*Alessio Ferrari,Minh An Nguyen,Kushal Ramkumar,Liliana Pasquale*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 一项针对26名网络安全硕士生的探索性研究显示，学生在漏洞修复任务中使用ChatGPT的策略差异不大，且与既有网络安全专长无关；但ChatGPT使用模式的多样性（即采用的不同使用模式数量）与作业成绩正相关，即使控制了专长因素后仍成立。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型（LLM）正快速进入软件工程教育，但其在"安全软件工程教育"中的作用仍缺乏研究。作者希望刻画学生在漏洞修复任务中如何实际使用ChatGPT，并检验使用模式与既有网络安全专长是否影响作业表现。

Method: 探索性实证研究。对象为某非全日制网络安全硕士项目中26名研究生，任务为漏洞修复作业。方法上收集学生的ChatGPT交互日志，采用结构化的双人编码（double-coding）程序对使用模式进行分类；随后分析使用模式、使用多样性（不同使用模式的数量）及先前网络安全专长与作业成绩的关联，并对多重比较进行校正，在控制专长的前提下检验多样性效应。

Result: 不同网络安全专长水平的学生采用了大致相似的ChatGPT使用策略。按成绩分组，个体使用模式呈现描述性差异，但经多重比较校正后均不具统计显著性。相反，ChatGPT使用多样性（即采用的不同使用模式数量）与成绩呈正相关，且在控制网络安全专长后依然显著。

Conclusion: 研究表明，学生"如何使用"ChatGPT可能比"是否使用"更具信息量，即使用模式的多样性比单一模式或专长更能解释作业表现。作为探索性结论，作者由此呼吁开展后续对照研究，以指导学生有效利用LLM进行安全软件工程学习。

Abstract: The rapid adoption of Large Language Models (LLMs) is reshaping software engineering education, but their role in secure software engineering education remains underexplored. We report an exploratory empirical study of how 26 graduate students in a part-time MSc Cybersecurity programme used ChatGPT during a vulnerability-fixing assignment. To characterise ChatGPT use, we analysed students' ChatGPT interaction logs using a structured double-coding procedure and examined whether usage patterns and prior cybersecurity expertise were associated with assignment performance. The results show that students with varying levels of cybersecurity expertise used broadly similar ChatGPT strategies. Individual usage patterns showed descriptive differences by grade, but none remained statistically significant after correcting for multiple comparisons. In contrast, diversity of ChatGPT usage, i.e., the number of distinct usage patterns adopted, was positively associated with performance, even after controlling for cybersecurity expertise. These exploratory findings suggest that the way students engage with ChatGPT may be more informative than whether they use it, and motivate future controlled studies to guide students toward effective LLM use in secure software engineering education.

</details>


### [91] [CoSTAR: Data Synthesis-Driven Constraint-Aware COBOL Section Summarization for Legacy System Modernization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11332)
*Hao Lin,He Jiang,Xiaochen Li,Weihong Sun,Yufu Wang,Zhilei Ren,Ang Jia*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: CoSTAR：执行验证数据合成+约束感知训练，使7B/8B小模型在COBOL段级摘要上媲美大型模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: COBOL对政府、金融机构及大型企业仍至关重要，但技术老化、专家流失与文档缺失使遗留系统现代化日益紧迫。迁移前需代码摘要以辅助理解，而COBOL段级摘要面临两大挑战：数据稀缺与迁移约束保持。

Method: 提出CoSTAR集成框架，将执行验证的数据合成与约束感知训练结合：复用通用编程任务，经由LLM生成合成执行验证的COBOL代码-摘要数据以缓解数据稀缺；在此基础上为目标段增强相关数据声明与自然语言解释，并以约束引导的结构化理由训练较小的基础LLM，使其保持COBOL段摘要的迁移约束。

Result: 在公开及保密企业COBOL系统上评估，合成3,764条执行验证训练实例；基于7B/8B基础LLM，ROUGE-L平均相对提升25.38%、METEOR 53.84%、chrF 37.22%。在企业真实评测中，仅基于Qwen3-8B的CoSTAR在准确性、完整性与简洁性上优于企业部署的Qwen3-235B。

Conclusion: CoSTAR使小型、可本地部署的LLM在隐私敏感的COBOL遗留系统上达到与远超其规模的大模型相竞争的性能。

Abstract: COBOL remains critical to governments, financial institutions, and large enterprises; yet, aging technologies, shrinking expertise, and missing documentation make modernization of COBOL-based legacy systems increasingly urgent. Before migration, code summarization is a common practice to support legacy system understanding. However, COBOL code summarization, especially on section-level, faces two key challenges: data scarcity and migration constraint preservation. To address these challenges, we propose CoSTAR, an integrated framework that combines execution-validated data synthesis with constraint-aware model training. CoSTAR repurposes general-purpose programming tasks to synthesize execution-validated COBOL code-summary data through LLM-based generation to overcome data scarcity. Based on the synthesized data, CoSTAR augments target sections with relevant data declarations and natural-language explanations, and uses constraint-guided structured rationales to train smaller base LLMs. The trained LLMs preserve the migration constraints for COBOL section summarization. We evaluate CoSTAR on both public and confidential enterprise COBOL systems. CoSTAR effectively synthesizes 3,764 execution-validated training instances. Based on these instances, CoSTAR built on 7B/8B base LLMs can improve these LLMs with average relative gains of 25.38% on ROUGE-L, 53.84% on METEOR, and 37.22% on chrF. In real-world enterprise evaluation, CoSTAR built on only Qwen3-8B, outperforms the enterprise-deployed Qwen3-235B in accuracy, completeness, and conciseness. These results show that CoSTAR enables small, locally deployable LLMs to achieve performance competitive with substantially larger LLMs for privacy-sensitive COBOL legacy systems.

</details>


### [92] [Agent-Integrated Software: Interaction Contracts and Continuous Assurance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11381)
*Shengcheng Yu,Chunrong Fang,Zhenyu Chen*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文提出 Agent-Integrated Software (AIS) 软件模式与 Intent-Level Interaction Abstraction (IIA) 任务语义，用以在应用内嵌智能体时建立任务级交互与应用行为之间的显式对应，并通过交互契约与持续保障来维持可信集成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在现有应用中嵌入智能体会产生持续性的协调问题：用户可在代理执行过程中修改目标、操作共享对象，导致任务级交互与应用行为脱节。作者认为可信集成需要二者之间显式的对应关系，而现有研究缺乏这一层面的抽象与保障机制。

Method: 采用立场/展望型研究：提出 AIS 模式（常规内核 + 直接交互 + 内置智能体）与 IIA 任务语义；构建开放转移系统模型，将 AIS 执行映射到 IIA 状态与事件；用交互契约（任务绑定、角色特定权限、控制转移、结果证据）约束该映射，并以持续保障在依赖变化时维护范围化断言；辅以精简披露契约与条件命题进行论证，并跨软件领域对比揭示假设与边界。

Result: 通过披露契约与条件命题说明局部组件有效性不足以支撑整体可信集成；论证可将部分准入不变量与规划过程分离，从而在受控执行中维持可信性。

Conclusion: 该视角提出一个跨应用抽象、开发支持、受控执行、质量评估与人类监督的研究议程，目标是使智能体集成成为可维护的软件工程学科。

Abstract: Embedding an intelligent agent in an existing application creates a persistent coordination problem: users can revise goals and manipulate shared objects while delegated execution continues. We argue that dependable integration requires an explicit correspondence between task-level interaction and application behavior. We introduce Agent-Integrated Software (AIS) as a software pattern combining a conventional core, direct interaction, and a built-in agent, and Intent-Level Interaction Abstraction (IIA) as the task semantics through which users inspect and control delegated work. An open transition-system model relates AIS execution to IIA states and events. Interaction contracts constrain this relation through task bindings, role-specific authority, control transitions, and outcome evidence; continuous assurance maintains scoped claims as their dependencies change. A compact disclosure contract and conditional propositions illustrate why local component validity is insufficient and how selected admission invariants can be separated from planning. Contrasting software domains expose the framework's assumptions and limits. This perspective develops a research agenda spanning application abstraction, development support, controlled execution, quality assessment, and human supervision, with the aim of making agent integration a maintainable software engineering discipline.

</details>


### [93] [Deep Learning-based Bug Triage System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11420)
*Sourabh Pal*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出基于RoBERTa-base的自动化缺陷分派（bug triage）系统，利用深度上下文表示对缺陷报告进行分类，5个训练轮次即达0.90的识别准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 缺陷分派是软件开发生命周期的关键环节，准确的分类与指派可提升开发效率；但人工分派成本高、易出错，因此需要自动化方案。

Method: 基于预训练RoBERTa-base Transformer架构微调，借助深度上下文语义表示对输入的缺陷报告进行自动分类，以优化指派流程。

Result: 实验评估显示，所提系统仅需5个训练轮次（epochs）即可达到0.90的缺陷识别准确率。

Conclusion: 微调后的Transformer模型在软件工程自动化任务中兼具高效率与高性能，具备实际应用价值。

Abstract: Effective bug triage is crucial for streamlining the software development lifecycle by accurately categorizing and assigning reported software defects. In this paper, we propose an automated bug triage system built upon the pre-trained RoBERTa-base transformer architecture. By leveraging deep contextual representations, our approach efficiently classifies incoming bug reports to optimize assignment. Experimental evaluation demonstrates that the proposed system achieves a strong bug identification accuracy of 0.90 within just five training epochs. These findings highlight the efficiency and high performance of fine-tuned transformer models for practical software engineering automation.

</details>


### [94] [ChurnBench: A Drift-Aware Benchmark Demonstrating That Refresh Scheduling, Not Cache Age, Governs Staleness in Agentic AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11515)
*Vivek Kumar Singh,Preeti Priyam*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出 ChurnBench，一个以时间线而非快照形式生成四源企业数据织物的开源基准，通过仅追加的 ground-truth 账本在每个时间戳解析金标答案，从而把"检索时正确、评估时错误"的时效性错误（freshness error）与推理错误区分开；实验表明计划性刷新下缓存年龄不能预测陈旧性，应扫描 TTL 配置与实体变化率的匹配而非漂移窗口长度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有检索基准冻结数据，只能判断智能体是否找到正确段落，无法判断其答案在当前时刻是否仍然为真；而生产环境中许可证重分配、用户离职、价格变动、合同续签等变化持续发生，导致答案可能"检索时正确、评估时已失效"，这类时效性错误缺乏可检测、可归因的评测手段。

Method: 1. 构建 ChurnBench：以时间线形式生成包含四个数据源的企业数据织物；2. 每次变更写入仅追加（append-only）的 ground-truth 账本，金标答案仅由账本计算、永不取自实际存储；3. 对每个 case 在检索时刻与评估时刻两个时间戳分别解析 ground truth，使"检索时正确但评估时错误"的答案被检出并标记为 freshness error，与 reasoning error 区分；4. 设计受控消融：在 1、14、28 天缓存年龄下对比启用/禁用分层刷新（tiered refresh）。

Result: 在计划性刷新系统中，缓存年龄不预测陈旧性：缓存年龄 1、14、28 天对应的时效性错误数分别为 7、4、4，因计划刷新以 TTL 界定陈旧上限，且各窗口均未观测到 TTL 失效。受控消融验证该机制：28 天时禁用分层刷新使时效性错误从 4 升至 45，而 1 天时结果完全不变。全部代码、评测框架及逐错误数据开源。

Conclusion: 漂移基准应扫描的变量是 TTL 配置与各实体变化率的匹配关系，而非漂移窗口长度；ChurnBench 提供了可复现、可归因的时效性评测工具，并全部开源。

Abstract: In production, agentic systems answer questions over data that lives in several places and keeps changing: licenses are reassigned, users offboarded, prices changed, contracts renewed. Existing retrieval benchmarks freeze the data, so they cannot ask whether an agent's answer is still true, only whether it found the right passage. We present ChurnBench, an open-source benchmark that generates a four-source enterprise data fabric as a timeline rather than a snapshot. Every change is written to an append-only ground-truth ledger, and gold answers are computed from that ledger, never from the live stores. An answer that was correct when its data was retrieved but wrong when evaluated is therefore detected and labeled a freshness error, distinct from a reasoning error; we validate this by resolving ground truth at both timestamps for every case reported. Using the instrument, we find that when a system refreshes on a schedule, cache age does not predict staleness. Across cache ages of 1, 14, and 28 days, freshness errors were 7, 4, and 4, because scheduled refresh bounds staleness by time-to-live, and no TTL lapse was observed in any window. A controlled ablation confirms the mechanism: disabling tiered refresh raises freshness errors from 4 to 45 at 28 days and leaves them identical at one day. The variable a drift benchmark should sweep is therefore TTL configuration against each entity's rate of change, not drift-window length. ChurnBench, the evaluation harness, and all per-error data are released open source.

</details>


### [95] [PRISMA-LLM: An Empirical Reporting Framework for AI-Assisted Systematic Reviews](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11559)
*Miguel Zabaleta,Baihan Lin*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该文基于SciLitBench语料库（888篇综述自动化论文、14,726条标注），刻画自动化方法、综述阶段使用、评估与局限性报告的变化，并提出PRISMA-LLM框架，将"实现披露"与"后果敏感型评估及局限性报告"分离。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM与AI软件日益参与系统综述决策，但审计这些流程所需的信息报告缺乏一致性，难以核查与问责，因此需要实证刻画现状并提供可操作的报告规范。

Method: 构建并分析SciLitBench语料库：888篇综述自动化论文、14,726条标注，统计方法演进、所涉综述阶段、评估方式与已报告局限性的分布特征。

Result: 自动化转向LLM与软件型工作流，并覆盖可改变证据基础的阶段；自2023年起，软件/产品类论文中38.0%未报告任何评估，而LLM类为9.3%；报告覆盖度随LLM工作流复杂度上升，但52%仅报告正向结果的LLM评估仍未满足某项可靠性或性能要求。

Conclusion: 报告质量不足且不均衡，尤其在软件/产品类工作流中；据此提出PRISMA-LLM框架，区分实现披露与后果敏感的评估、局限性报告，以支持系统综述自动化流程的可审计性。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) and AI-enabled software increasingly participate in systematic-review decisions, yet the information needed to audit these workflows is reported inconsistently. We analyze SciLitBench, a corpus of 888 review-automation papers with 14,726 annotations, to characterize changes in methods, review-stage use, evaluation and reported limitations. Automation has shifted toward LLM- and software-facing workflows, including stages that can alter the evidence base. Since 2023, 38.0% of software/product papers reported no evaluation, compared with 9.3% of LLM papers. Reporting coverage increased with LLM workflow complexity, yet 52% of positive-only LLM evaluations still reported an unmet reliability or performance requirement. From these patterns, we introduce PRISMA-LLM, an empirically grounded framework separating implementation disclosure from consequence-sensitive evaluation and limitation reporting.

</details>


### [96] [Ecdysis: Efficient and Effective Training of Runtime Harnesses for LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11677)
*Ruiqing Yue,Yu Cui,Zhuoyu Sun,Sicheng Pan,Xianhong Xue,Tingyu Li,Ting Li,Wenzhuo Zhu,Yi Chen,Yifei Liu,Baohan Huang,Zhe Cui,Haibin Zhang,Cong Zuo*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: Ecdysis：通过跨实例失败聚合与多角色诊断实现高效、有效的自演化harness框架


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有harness演化方法依赖迭代搜索，反复执行与修改候选harness，导致时间开销大，且易过拟合于已观测任务与特定失败模式，泛化性下降。作者指出缺乏原则性失败诊断是关键瓶颈：单次失败可能源于模型自身缺陷或系统性harness缺陷，直接针对个体失败优化会引入不必要的模型特化适配。

Method: Ecdysis区分"模型特化适配"与"harness层级修复"，将适配偏向系统性harness缺陷。采用batch-level跨实例失败聚合范式，联合分析多个任务实例的失败证据；引入Failure-Driven Collaborative Refinement，通过多角色诊断失败原因并迭代精化harness修改规格。

Result: 与现有harness演化方法相比，harness训练最高实现1.84倍加速，同时所得harness的推理准确率提升18.56%。

Conclusion: 跨实例失败分析与多角色诊断相结合，使Ecdysis在更低训练时间下实现更有效的harness演化，兼顾效率与泛化性能。

Abstract: Self-evolving runtime harnesses can substantially improve the capabilities of large language model (LLM) agents and provide a promising paradigm for optimizing agent execution. Existing harness evolution methods typically rely on iterative search, repeatedly evaluating and revising candidate harnesses based on execution feedback from task instances. While this paradigm enables continuous harness optimization, it incurs substantial time overhead due to repeated agent executions and code modifications, and may overfit to observed tasks and specific failure patterns, resulting in degraded generalization to unseen tasks. We identify the lack of principled failure diagnosis as a key bottleneck in harness evolution: an observed failure can reflect either model-specific deficiencies or systematic harness deficiencies, and directly optimizing against individual failures can lead to unnecessary model-specific accommodation. We therefore propose Ecdysis, an efficient and effective framework that distinguishes model-specific accommodation from harness-level repair and biases adaptation toward systematic harness deficiencies by identifying recurring cross-task failure patterns. Ecdysis adopts a batch-level cross-instance failure aggregation paradigm to jointly analyze failure evidence from multiple task instances and further introduces Failure-Driven Collaborative Refinement to diagnose failure causes and iteratively refine harness modification specifications. By combining cross-instance failure analysis with multi-role diagnosis, Ecdysis enables more effective harness evolution with lower training time. Experiments show that Ecdysis achieves up to a 1.84x speedup in harness training compared with existing harness evolution methods, while improving the reasoning accuracy of the resulting harnesses by 18.56%.

</details>


### [97] [Reproducibility in the Age of Agentic AI: Context Engineering at the Timescale of a Codebase](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11728)
*Lorena A. Barba*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该文主张可复现研究实践本质上是对AI编码智能体的"上下文工程"：智能体降低了测试、提交历史、仓库结构、指令与决策记录等工件的维护成本，使其收益即时显现；但研究者仍须负责核验这些工件及其所编码的科学判断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可复现研究实践长期面临维护成本高、收益滞后的问题，导致研究者投入意愿不足；同时AI编码智能体的兴起改变了科研中工件维护的成本—收益结构，亟需重新定位其角色与责任边界。

Method: 概念性论证（观点性/立场性论文）：将可复现研究实践重新概念化为面向AI编码智能体的上下文工程，从成本与收益两个维度分析智能体对测试、提交历史、仓库结构、指令文件和决策记录等工件的影响。

Result: 智能体显著降低了上述可复现工件的维护成本，并使其收益变得更即时、更直接（因智能体本身依赖这些上下文来有效工作）。

Conclusion: 可复现实践在AI编码智能体时代具有新的正当性与紧迫性；但责任不可外包——研究者仍需核验工件本身及其所承载的科学判断。

Abstract: Reproducible research practices are context engineering for AI coding agents. I argue that agents lower the cost of maintaining tests, commit histories, repository structure, instructions, and decision records while making their benefits immediate. Researchers remain responsible for verifying these artifacts and the scientific judgments they encode.

</details>


### [98] [An analysis of the relationship of input metrics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11824)
*Addison Crump*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文系统比较了划分测试中的多种输入度量指标，指出传统经验比较策略不足，提出k-alt-path新度量以降低冗余并提升敏感度，并为未来输入度量改进建立分析框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 输入度量用于评估测试进度，自20世纪50年代起已有多种此类度量被提出，但鲜有研究对其进行系统比较；同时，典型经验比较策略存在根本性不足，需建立更严谨的比较与改进方法。

Method: 借助划分测试文献中针对其他度量类别提出的现有方法，先定义并回顾常见输入度量；通过案例研究揭示典型经验比较策略的不足；随后定义并实现k-alt-path新度量，以降低冗余并提升对k-path的敏感度；最后系统比较其余常见输入度量。

Result: 案例研究表明典型经验比较策略对度量比较根本不足；k-alt-path相较k-path能减少冗余并提高敏感度；对其余常见输入度量的系统比较揭示了各度量特性及其含义。

Conclusion: 本文提出划分测试分析方法，为输入度量的严谨比较与改进提供依据，并形成未来研究完善输入度量的策略。

Abstract: Input metrics evaluate the progress of testing in terms of features of inputs present in a test suite. Previous works, as early as the 1950s, established a number of such metrics, but few endeavored to compare them. This paper does so by utilizing existing methods proposed for other metric classes in partition testing literature. After defining and reviewing common input metrics, we begin with a short case study revealing that typical empirical comparison strategies are fundamentally insufficient for comparing metrics. Then, we demonstrate how one rigorously improves a standard metric by defining and implementing $k$-alt-path, a new metric which reduces redundancy while improving sensitivity over $k$-path. Each of the other common input metrics are then systematically compared before discussing the implications of our findings. With these contributions, we bring forward partition testing analysis methods that justify and form a strategy for future research in refining input metrics.

</details>


<div id='cs.IR'></div>

# cs.IR [[Back]](#toc)

### [99] [When Synthetic Data Hurts: On Catastrophic Forgetting in Skill Retrieval for LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10750)
*Syed Shariyar Murtaza,Yifan Nie,Utkarsh Soni,Eugene Wen,Arvid Frydenlund*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 该论文面向 LLM agent 的大规模技能选择问题，构建了覆盖 34,396 个技能的生产级技能路由器，并研究了在真实监督稀缺、主要依赖合成数据场景下的技能检索与微调策略。研究发现合成数据微调虽提升同分布检索，却导致对真实与 OOD 数据的灾难性遗忘；引入多种持续学习式遗忘缓解方法后，可在保持 OOD 检索性能的同时将合成同分布技能检索提升 13.98%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM agent 越来越多依赖运行时检索的外部技能，使得从大规模技能库中进行技能选择成为关键挑战。然而真实监督信号稀缺，实践中往往依赖合成数据微调；这种微调可能带来分布外性能退化，因此需要研究如何在有限真实监督与合成数据条件下实现稳健的技能检索。

Method: 作者构建了一个覆盖 34,396 个技能的生产级技能路由器，并开展大规模技能检索研究，训练数据由有限真实监督与合成数据组成。针对合成数据微调引发的灾难性遗忘，评估了多种受持续学习启发的遗忘缓解微调方法，包括 embedding-anchor 正则化、Learning without Forgetting (LwF)、Elastic Weight Consolidation (EWC) 以及 L2-initialization；实验模型为 0.6B Qwen retriever 与 reranker。

Result: 合成数据微调能够提升同分布检索效果，但会造成在真实数据和 OOD 数据上的灾难性遗忘。上述遗忘缓解方法不仅保留了 OOD 技能检索性能，还将合成同分布技能检索效果提升了 13.98%（针对 0.6B Qwen retriever 和 reranker）。

Conclusion: 该工作为真实监督稀缺、多正例监督条件下的技能检索提供了实用的基准与稳健的微调方案，表明持续学习式遗忘缓解策略可有效兼顾同分布提升与 OOD 泛化。

Abstract: LLM agents increasingly rely on external skills retrieved at runtime, making skill selection from large repositories a critical challenge. We present a production skill router over 34,396 skills and a large-scale study of skill retrieval using limited real supervision and synthetic data. We found that the synthetic-data fine-tuning improves in-distribution retrieval but it causes catastrophic forgetting on real and out-of-distribution (OOD) data. We evaluate several forgetting mitigation fine-tuning approaches inspired by continual learning, including embedding-anchor regularization, Learning without Forgetting (LwF), Elastic Weight Consolidation (EWC), and L2-initialization. The results show that these approaches not only retain the performance on OOD skills retrieval but also improve the retrieval on synthetic in-distribution skills by 13.98\% for 0.6B Qwen retriever and reranker. Our results provide a practical benchmark and a robust fine-tuning recipe for scarce, multi-positive supervision.

</details>


### [100] [UniRec: Cross-stage Multi-Task Fusion with Preference Alignment for Cascaded Recommender Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11052)
*Lingyuan Kong,Jiaqi Cui,Fanjiao Zeng,Congqi Wang,Yu Li,Yuan Cheng,Jingxin Liu,Xiaoshuang Chen,Kaiqiao Zhan*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出 UniRec 统一跨阶段推荐融合模型，通过共享嵌入、单计算图训练与双轴偏好对齐解决预排序与排序阶段的跨阶段不一致，并引入属性组相对正则抑制分布失衡，离线优于基线，在线 App 使用时长提升 0.616%，已在快手全量部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业推荐系统采用级联多阶段（不同目标、特征空间与时延约束）。预排序与排序独立优化会导致跨阶段不一致：上游可能过滤掉下游排序偏好的物料，下游独立调优的融合又会抵消上游改进。现有工作局限：多任务融合多聚焦排序阶段内部的多目标融合；跨阶段方法通常只在上游评分中加入下游分数因子；两阶段融合模块的联合优化基本未被探索。

Method: 1）两个融合代理部分共享输入嵌入，并在单一计算图中训练，使任一阶段的梯度经共享表示传播并影响另一阶段。2）双轴偏好对齐目标：纵向跨阶段一致性项将下游成对偏好迁移到上游融合分数；横向紧凑聚合项将异构先验信号上的数十个成对目标重组为双向偏好证据。3）发现无约束端到端融合会利用物料属性分布不均衡，过度集中于高收益区域而牺牲其他目标，故加入属性组相对正则：在属性组内计算优势并对同组归一化策略，使整体偏向某个高收益组不产生优化增益。

Result: 离线实验中，UniRec 稳定优于单阶段融合与跨阶段协调基线；在线 A/B 测试显示 App 使用时长提升 0.616%；已在快手平台完成全量部署。

Conclusion: 跨阶段融合模块的联合优化可有效缓解级联推荐系统的阶段不一致问题；配合属性组相对正则，UniRec 在离线与线上均取得一致收益，并具备大规模工业落地的可行性。

Abstract: Industrial recommender systems use cascaded stages with different objectives, feature spaces, and latency constraints. Optimizing pre-ranking and ranking separately can create cross-stage inconsistency: upstream models may filter out items preferred by downstream rankers, and independently tuned downstream fusion can offset upstream improvements. Existing multi-task fusion methods focus on multi-objective fusion within the ranking stage, and cross-stage methods typically only add a downstream score factor to upstream ranking. Joint optimization of fusion modules across both stages remains largely unexplored.
  We propose UniRec, a Unified Cross-stage Recommendation Fusion model. First, the two fusion agents partially share input embeddings and are trained in a single computation graph, so gradients from either stage propagate through the shared representation and influence the other. Second, we introduce a dual-axis preference alignment objective: a vertical cross-stage consistency term transfers downstream pairwise preferences to the upstream fusion score, and a horizontal compact aggregation term reorganizes dozens of pairwise objectives over heterogeneous prior signals into bidirectional preference evidence. Third, we find that unconstrained end-to-end fusion optimization can exploit imbalances in item attribute distributions, over-concentrating on high-reward regions at the cost of other objectives. We therefore add an attribute group-relative regularization that computes advantages within attribute groups and normalizes the policy over the same groups, so uniformly promoting an entire high-reward group yields no optimization gain. Offline, UniRec consistently outperforms single-stage fusion and cross-stage coordination baselines. Online A/B tests show a 0.616\% gain in app usage duration. UniRec is fully deployed on the Kuaishou platform.

</details>


### [101] [VikingRAG: Accurate and Token-efficient Retrieval-augmented Generation over Structured Documents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11390)
*Peiyuan Gao,Gaoyuan Zhang,Haojie Qin,Yahui Sun,Qianyi Zhang,Yunhao Zhang,Zeyu Wang,Wei Lu*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出 VikingRAG，一种目录感知的语义数据管理系统，通过语义与结构访问的紧耦合、检索轨迹复用和自适应升级策略，在保持高 RAG 准确率的同时大幅降低 token 开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 SOTA 的检索增强生成（RAG）方法虽借助文档结构获取充分证据，但常带来较高的 token 成本；如何在保证高 RAG 准确率的前提下减少结构性上下文 token，是本文的核心动机。

Method: VikingRAG 是一个目录感知（directory-aware）的语义数据管理系统，紧耦合语义与结构访问，支持结构高效、以证据缺口（evidence-gap）驱动的多轮检索；将智能体式多轮检索轨迹物化为经验边（experience edges），供相似查询复用，避免重复的多轮探索；并引入自适应升级策略，在证据充分时仅用一轮经验增强检索作答，否则才触发智能体式多轮检索。

Result: 在真实数据集上，基础系统 VikingRAG 在达到 SOTA 方法高准确率的同时，仅消耗其 11.6%–51.9% 的 token；结合检索轨迹复用与自适应升级后，token 成本降至 5.1%–32.5%，并保持有竞争力的准确率与实用的文档存储性能。

Conclusion: 结果表明该方法对新兴的 AI 知识库具有实用价值。

Abstract: State-of-the-art retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) methods exploit document structures to acquire sufficient evidence, but often incur substantial token costs. To reduce structural-context tokens without compromising high RAG accuracy, we present {\sf VikingRAG}, a directory-aware semantic data management system that tightly integrates semantic and structural access to support structural-context-efficient, evidence-gap-driven multi-round retrieval. To further reduce token overhead of multi-round interaction, we materialize agentic multi-round retrieval traces as experience edges, and reuse these edges for similar queries, avoiding repeated multi-round exploration. To additionally reduce token costs when agentic multi-round retrieval is unnecessary, we introduce an adaptive escalation strategy that answers from one-round experience-augmented retrieval when the evidence is sufficient, and invokes agentic multi-round retrieval only otherwise. Experiments on real datasets show that the base system {\sf VikingRAG} matches high accuracy of state-of-the-art methods while consuming only 11.6\%--51.9\% of their tokens. With retrieval-trace reuse and adaptive escalation, token costs drop to 5.1\%--32.5\% while maintaining competitive accuracy and practical document-storage performance, showing the utility of this work for emerging AI knowledge bases.

</details>


### [102] [TimelyRAG: Semantic-Temporal Hybrid Retrieval for Time-Critical Question Answering in Overlapping-Evolving Documents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11572)
*Youngeun Nam,Joeun Kim,Hwanjun Song,Susik Yoon,Jae-Gil Lee,Byung Suk Lee*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 该论文提出TimelyRAG框架，将时间距离引入检索排序，以解决文档在重叠式演化（修订覆盖旧条款但保留大部分内容）场景下的问答不可靠问题，并发布首个面向重叠演化挑战的基准TimelyQABench，实验显示nDCG@10最高提升28.6%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型与检索增强生成在开放域问答上虽有进展，但在文档经修订演化时仍不可靠。现有时间敏感检索方法仅针对"分离式演化"（每次更新为独立快照），而法律、政策、法规常处于"重叠式演化"环境：修订覆盖早期条款却保留大部分内容，导致版本间存在强语义重叠，现有方法难以应对。

Method: 提出TimelyRAG，一个与检索器无关（retriever-agnostic）的框架，将时间距离（temporal distance）纳入排序过程，使查询与版本适配的文档对齐；同时构建TimelyQABench，首个针对重叠演化挑战的法规密集型领域基准。

Result: 实验取得一致增益，nDCG@10最高提升28.6%，表明时间推理对于在演化文档上进行可靠问答具有重要意义。

Conclusion: 时间推理是应对重叠式演化文档问答的关键；TimelyRAG与TimelyQABench为法规等重叠演化领域的可靠问答提供了有效框架与评测基准，所有资源已开源。

Abstract: Although large language models (LLMs) and retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) have advanced open-domain question answering (QA), they remain unreliable when documents evolve through amendments. Existing time-sensitive retrieval methods address only the disjoint-evolving environment, where each update is an independent snapshot. However, laws, policies, and regulations often operate in overlapping-evolving environments, where amendments override earlier clauses while preserving most content, creating strong semantic overlap across versions. We propose TimelyRAG, a retriever-agnostic framework that incorporates temporal distance into ranking to align queries with version-appropriate documents. We also introduce TimelyQABench, the first benchmark for regulation-heavy domains with overlapping-evolving challenges. Experiments show consistent gains, up to +28.6% in nDCG@10, highlighting the importance of temporal reasoning for reliable QA over evolving documents. All resources are available at https://github.com/kaist-dmlab/TimelyRAG.

</details>


### [103] [FedHUR: Learning Hierarchical Utility-Guided Client Relations for Personalized Federated Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11632)
*Mingzhe Han,Jiahao Liu,Dongsheng Li,Jiankui Zhou,Hansu Gu,Peng Zhang,Ning Gu,Tun Lu*
