Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出FedHUR框架，以物品-物品过滤器为对象，通过学习分层效用引导的客户端关系实现联邦推荐中的个性化聚合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有联邦推荐个性化聚合方法依赖预设的基于参数的假设（如参数相似性或互补性）构建客户端关系，且只构建单一全局关系，难以刻画推荐中用户关系的层次性与多粒度特性；同时这些预设关系无法直接反映相关客户端聚合后是否真能提升预测性能。

Method: FedHUR以item-item filters为关系构建与聚合对象：首先对各客户端本地信息进行聚合与聚类，获得全局分层信息；每个客户端基于本地信息与全局分层信息计算分层效用信号，指示哪些协作信息有助于提升其预测；服务器利用这些效用信号检索对该客户端有用的客户端，进行进一步的个性化聚合。

Result: 在五个真实数据集上的大量实验表明，FedHUR持续优于现有联邦推荐基线，验证了分层效用引导的客户端关系学习的有效性。代码已开源。

Conclusion: FedHUR通过分层效用引导的客户端关系学习，克服了预设单一全局关系的局限，实现了更有效的个性化联邦推荐聚合。

Abstract: Federated recommendation enables collaborative model training while keeping user interaction data on local clients. A central problem in federated recommendation is how to aggregate useful information across clients for personalized recommendation. Existing personalized aggregation methods usually construct client relations from predefined parameter-based assumptions, such as parameter similarity or complementarity, and use these relations to determine aggregation weights. However, such methods construct a single global relation, which is insufficient to capture the hierarchical and multi-granularity nature of user relations in recommendation. Moreover, these predefined relations cannot directly reflect whether the related clients can improve prediction performance after aggregation. To address these limitations, we propose FedHUR, a federated recommendation framework for learning hierarchical utility-guided client relations. FedHUR takes item-item filters as the object for relation construction and aggregation. Specifically, it first aggregates and clusters each client's local information to obtain global hierarchical information. Each client computes hierarchical utility signals based on its local information and the global hierarchical information, indicating which collaborative information is useful for improving its prediction. The server uses these utility signals to retrieve clients that are useful to that client for further personalized aggregation. Extensive experiments on five real-world datasets show that FedHUR consistently outperforms existing federated recommendation baselines, demonstrating the effectiveness of hierarchical utility-guided client relation learning. Code is available at https://github.com/Mingzhe-Han/FedHUR.

</details>


### [104] [Your Retriever Already Knows: Distribution-Shape QPP for RAG Retrieval Sufficiency](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11646)
*Matyáš Veselý,Michal Průšek,Jiří Franc*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文比较三种查询性能预测（QPP）范式用于RAG检索充分性判断：基于分数特征、基于内容的LLM评判、以及二者混合。提出的GeneralQPP（24个非词汇特征）在ViDoRe八个视觉域上AUROC达0.856，每查询2ms，优于经典QPP池（0.835）并远超本地LLM评判（0.649），速度快约3000倍；混合方法在SÚJB监管数据集上取得显著优势（AUROC 0.911，对抗检测0.954）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准RAG管线在推理时缺乏检索成功与否的可靠信号，面对歧义或超出范围的查询时生成容易产生幻觉。作者受捷克核监管机构部署场景驱动——该场景数据敏感性禁止使用第三方LLM API，因此需要本地、低成本且有效的检索充分性预测方法。

Method: 在八个ViDoRe视觉域（14,514条查询）上比较三类QPP范式：(1)基于分数的特征方法，即GeneralQPP，包含24个非词汇特征（15个分布形状、5个查询表面、4个全局特征）；(2)基于内容的本地LLM评判（Qwen3.5）；(3)混合方法，将LLM判断作为特征之一。另在SÚJB数据集（1,510条查询，含500条合成对抗查询）上评估，并采用Leave-One-Domain-Out（LODO）协议及逐步特征选择。

Result: GeneralQPP在ViDoRe上加权平均AUROC为0.856，每查询2ms，优于经典QPP文献池（Classic Full，0.835），并远超本地Qwen3.5 LLM评判（0.649，差距+0.207，速度快且便宜约3000倍）。混合方法在ViDoRe上与S1持平（0.863），但在SÚJB上获得统计显著优势（Hit@5时AUROC 0.911，对抗检测0.954），代价是LLM延迟。跨数据集排名一致（Spearman ρ=0.90）。LODO下S1降至0.706，13特征逐步子集S1-Lean恢复至0.719（比文献池高+0.032）。

Conclusion: 基于分数的轻量非词汇QPP特征（GeneralQPP）在检索充分性预测上以极低延迟和成本大幅优于LLM评判，适合数据敏感、禁止第三方API的部署环境。混合LLM特征仅在特定监管数据集上带来显著增益但引入延迟；在跨域泛化（LODO）下性能下降，需通过特征裁剪（S1-Lean）部分恢复，说明跨域鲁棒性仍是挑战。

Abstract: Standard Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) pipelines often provide no reliable inference-time signal of whether retrieval succeeded; on ambiguous or out-of-scope queries, generation may then hallucinate. Motivated by a Czech nuclear-regulator deployment where data sensitivity precludes third-party LLM APIs, we compare three Query Performance Prediction (QPP) paradigms for retrieval sufficiency in RAG: score-based features, a content-based LLM judge, and a hybrid. On the eight ViDoRe vision domains (14,514 queries), our 24 non-lexical features (GeneralQPP; 15 distribution-shape, 5 query-surface, 4 global) reach a weighted-average AUROC of 0.856 at 2 ms per query, ahead of a classic-QPP literature pool (Classic Full, 0.835) and well above a local Qwen3.5 LLM judge (0.649, +0.207 gap; $\sim$3000$\times$ faster and cheaper per query). Adding the LLM judgment as one feature (hybrid) matches S1 on ViDoRe (0.863) but gains a statistically significant edge on SÚJB (AUROC 0.911 at Hit@5, adversarial-detection 0.954; 1,510 queries, 500 synthetic adversarial), at LLM latency. Rankings agree across datasets (Spearman $ρ= 0.90$). Under Leave-One-Domain-Out, S1 drops to 0.706; a 13-feature LODO-stepwise subset (S1-Lean) recovers to 0.719 (+0.032 over the literature pool).

</details>


### [105] [Generative Late-Interaction Embeddings For Visual Document Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11808)
*Mohamed Eltahir,Talal Aloushan,Rose Khairoalsendi,Jana Shata,Mohammed Alhassan,Leen Alrehaili,Tanveer Hussain,Naeemullah Khan*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: GLIE通过利用向量位于单位球面且集中于内在维度5-6的流形的几何性质，用少量（k≪N）学习向量同时作为轻量索引和生成基，查询时只搜索这k个向量，再对top候选用解码器还原全部N个向量做精确重打分。ViDoRe v1上每页4向量保留近80% nDCG@5（最优先前方法70%），仅需415K参数、不到3 GPU分钟、1000训练页。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 晚期交互检索（late-interaction）是视觉文档搜索的SOTA，但每页约1000个向量的存储代价高昂。现有压缩方法只保留子集或局部平均，在激进存储预算下精度急剧下降，且替代方案需重训练编码器。作者希望在不重训练编码器的前提下实现存储高效的晚期交互检索。

Method: 分析三种编码器发现两个一致性质：向量精确位于单位球面上，且集中在一个内在维度为5-6的流形附近。据此提出两点：①标准k-means质心落在球内，导致MaxSim分数系统性低估，将其归一化到球面是免费修正，最多带来+0.093 nDCG@5；②因流形自由度低，全量向量可由少数向量再生。据此提出GLIE：每页用k≪N个从归一化质心学习得到的向量，既作轻量索引又作再生基。查询时仅在这k个向量上搜索，解码器只把top候选扩展回全部N个向量做精确重打分。

Result: 在ViDoRe v1上每页4个向量时，GLIE保留未压缩系统nDCG@5的近80%，而最优先验后处理方法仅70%。系统使用415K参数的网络，在仅1000训练页上不到3 GPU分钟拟合完成。在相同训练预算下，微调编码器甚至达不到GLIE的无训练阶段，且在各预算下完整系统均优于微调。

Conclusion: GLIE通过按需重建证据而非采样证据，开辟了存储高效检索的新方向，解码器成为主要设计面。该结果在第二种编码器及ViDoRe v2上同样成立。

Abstract: Late-interaction retrieval is the state-of-the-art for visual document search, but it pays for its accuracy in storage. Existing compression methods retain a subset or local average of the N~1,000 vectors per page. Under aggressive storage budgets, however, these methods degrade sharply, and alternatives require retraining the encoder. Investigating this degradation across three encoders, we found two consistent properties: the vectors lie exactly on the unit sphere and concentrate near a manifold of intrinsic dimension five to six. This geometry yields two insights. First, standard k-means centroids fall inside the sphere, causing systematic underestimation of MaxSim scores. Normalizing them to the surface is a free correction worth up to +0.093 nDCG@5 over raw centroids. Second, because the page manifold has few degrees of freedom, the full set of vectors can be regenerated from only a few. To this end, we introduce Generative Late-Interaction Embeddings (GLIE): k << N vectors per page learned from the normalized centroids to serve as both a lightweight index and a basis for regenerating the page's full embedding set. At query time, search runs exclusively on these k vectors, and a decoder expands only the top candidates back to all N vectors for exact rescoring. At four vectors per page on ViDoRe v1, GLIE retains nearly 80% of the uncompressed system's nDCG@5, against 70% for the best prior post-hoc method. These results use a 415K-parameter network fitted in under three GPU-minutes on just a thousand training pages. At a matched training budget, fine-tuning the encoder does not reach even the training-free stage of GLIE, and the full system beats it at every budget. These patterns hold across a second encoder and ViDoRe v2. By reconstructing evidence on demand rather than sampling it, GLIE opens a new axis for storage-efficient retrieval, with the decoder as its main design surface.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [106] [Probabilistic Focal Search: Accelerating Bounded-Suboptimal Search via Lower-Bound Advancement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10584)
*Minh Vu Duc,Trung Le Huu,Hà Minh Hoàng,Trung Thanh Nguyen,Phuong Khanh Nguyen,Huynh Thi Thanh Binh*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出概率焦点搜索(PFS)，通过以概率p执行引导选择、以1-p扩展最小f节点，加速下界推进并扩大FOCAL，从而降低有界次优搜索代价。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 焦点搜索(FS)在有界次优搜索中虽用FOCAL内的启发式引导，但其确定性策略可能导致f_min长时间不变，延迟FOCAL接纳，成为搜索瓶颈。

Method: PFS以概率p遵循FS引导选择，以概率1-p扩展OPEN中最小f节点；并将该调度器迁移至Dynamic Potential Search得到PDPS；进一步构造随时扩展APFS。

Result: 在N-Puzzle、Pancake Sorting、TSP上对比FS，并在GCTSP上评测随时版本。当f_min长期停滞时收益最大，节点扩展可减少约90%以上；APFS在GCTSP随时评测中优于所有测试算法；Pancake Sorting等确定性搜索已高效时收益较小；PDPS表明机制可迁移至potential引导，但效果依赖领域与界。

Conclusion: 概率因子在FOCAL接纳成为瓶颈时最有效，通过平衡启发式引导与下界推进显著提升有界次优搜索效率，并具一定可迁移性。

Abstract: Bounded-suboptimal search seeks a solution within a factor $w$ of optimal while reducing search effort. Focal Search (FS) uses heuristic guidance within FOCAL, the frontier nodes eligible under the threshold $w f_{\min}$, but its deterministic policy may leave $f_{\min}$ unchanged for many expansions. We introduce Probabilistic Focal Search (PFS), which follows the FS guided choice with probability $p$ and expands a minimum-$f$ OPEN node with probability $1-p$. The latter branch encourages the lower bound to advance, enlarging FOCAL and admitting nodes that may lead to feasible solutions. By balancing guidance and lower-bound advancement, this mechanism can reduce time to a bounded solution when progress is limited by delayed FOCAL admission. As a secondary transfer experiment, we apply the same scheduler to Dynamic Potential Search, yielding Probabilistic Dynamic Potential Search (PDPS). We benchmark PFS against FS on N-Puzzle, Pancake Sorting, and the Traveling Salesperson Problem (TSP), and evaluate its anytime extension on the Generalized Covering TSP (GCTSP), using multiple $w$ and $p$ values. Across these benchmarks, the largest gains occur when long $f_{\min}$ plateaus delay useful FOCAL admissions; in such settings, the probabilistic factor may reduce node expansions by about 90\% or more (e.g., on N-Puzzle and TSP). For the anytime algorithm family, Anytime Probabilistic Focal Search (APFS) outperforms all tested algorithms in evaluating anytime methods on GCTSP. We also observe that the benefit is smaller when the deterministic search already advances efficiently (e.g., Pancake Sorting), indicating that the probabilistic factor is most useful when FOCAL admission is a search bottleneck. The PDPS transfer shows that the mechanism also transfers to potential guidance, although its common-success effects remain domain- and bound-dependent.

</details>


### [107] [Automating Quadratic Unconstrained Binary Optimization (QUBO) Formulation Generation from Natural Language](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10629)
*Niloy Kumar Mondal,Md Rizwan Parvez*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出一个端到端多智能体框架，可将自然语言问题描述自动转换为QUBO建模，并发布涵盖12个领域、100个问题的QUBOBench基准，实现68%准确率，比单次调用基线高22%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: QUBO是组合优化的核心建模形式，但将自然语言问题描述转化为正确的QUBO建模十分困难，需要识别二进制变量、约束、目标函数、惩罚项及合适的惩罚权重，过程耗时且依赖大量领域专业知识。

Method: 提出端到端多智能体框架，从自然语言问题描述自动生成QUBO建模，并支持结构化或非结构化测试用例。同时构建QUBOBench基准，包含12个应用领域、100个组合优化问题，问题源自同行评审文献、竞赛及经典NP-hard问题。在实验中与直接单次调用基线进行对比，并通过进一步分析定位关键组件。

Result: 框架在QUBOBench上达到68%的准确率，比直接单次调用基线高出22%；进一步分析表明，迭代自修复是提升性能的最重要组成部分。数据与代码已开源。

Conclusion: 该多智能体框架能有效降低QUBO建模门槛，在多种组合优化问题上显著优于单次调用基线，其中迭代自修复机制是性能提升的关键；配套基准与开源资源为后续研究提供了统一评测基础。

Abstract: Quadratic Unconstrained Binary Optimization (QUBO) is a central formulation for combinatorial optimization and has gained increasing attention due to its compatibility with quantum, hybrid quantum-classical, and quantum-inspired solvers. However, translating natural-language problem descriptions into correct QUBO formulations remains difficult, requiring the identification of binary variables, constraints, objective functions, penalty terms, and suitable penalty weights. This process is time-consuming and often demands substantial domain expertise. To address this challenge, we propose an end-to-end multi-agent framework that automatically generates QUBO formulations from natural-language problem descriptions, supported by structured or unstructured test cases. To evaluate its performance, We also introduce QUBOBench, a benchmark containing 100 combinatorial optimization problems across 12 application domains, curated from peer-reviewed literature, competitions, and canonical NP-hard problems. Experimental results show that our framework achieves 68% accuracy on QUBOBench, outperforming a direct single-call baseline by 22%. Further analysis identifies iterative self-repair as the most important component contributing to improved performance. The data and code are open-sourced at https://quitttcat.github.io/QuantumQUBOAgent.

</details>


### [108] [A Multi-Stage Rule-Chaining Framework for Compositional and Interpretable Cognitive Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10654)
*Deblina Kar*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出多阶段规则链式框架，跨符号、结构、概念三级做组合推理求解ARC，整体准确率超95%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ARC用于评测认知泛化能力，即从少量示例中推断并应用抽象规则；现有方法泛化性与可解释性不足，且多依赖任务特定调优。

Method: 框架集成三个互补求解器并按渐进式回退层级顺序运行：(1)确定性规则发现模块，通过几何、颜色与对象分析归纳原子变换；(2)模式组合引擎，借助块合并、重复与空间启发式重建输出；(3)结构抽象层，推断网格间的层级与嵌套关系。各阶段复用前序推理轨迹以增强可解释性与泛化。

Result: 训练集1000题中通过995题；在120题中评测105题；ARC-AGI-2的240题测试中解出230题。在确定性、组合性、抽象性类别上覆盖良好，整体准确率超过95%。

Conclusion: 该架构衔接符号推理与模式合成，为认知泛化提供可解释视角；表明规则链式与层级组合可在不依赖任务特定调优的情况下，推动机器推理走向透明、与人类对齐的抽象。

Abstract: The Abstraction and Reasoning Corpus (ARC) benchmarks cognitive generalization, the ability to infer and apply abstract rules from limited examples. This paper presents a multi-stage rule-chaining framework that performs compositional reasoning across symbolic, structural, and conceptual levels. The framework integrates three complementary solvers:
  (1) a deterministic rule discovery module that induces atomic transformations through geometric, color, and object-based analysis;
  (2) a pattern-composition engine that reconstructs outputs via block merging, repetition, and spatial heuristics; and
  (3) a structural abstraction layer that infers hierarchical and nested relationships across grids.
  These solvers operate sequentially within a progressive fallback hierarchy, where each stage reuses prior reasoning traces to enhance interpretability and generalization. Training passed for 995 tasks out of 1000, further evaluated on 105 tasks out of 120 and solved 230 test tasks out of 240 ARC-AGI-2 tasks. The system achieved strong coverage across deterministic, compositional, and abstract categories, demonstrating an overall accuracy exceeding 95 percent. The proposed architecture bridges symbolic reasoning and pattern synthesis, providing interpretable insight into cognitive generalization. The results suggest that rule chaining and hierarchical composition can advance machine reasoning toward transparent, human-aligned abstraction without relying on task-specific tuning.

</details>


### [109] [Understanding LoRA Rank Trade-offs in Diffusion Model Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10656)
*Iman Khazrak,Narges Nejad,Mostafa M. Rezaee,Robert C. Green*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 在CIFAR-10上系统研究LoRA秩对扩散模型微调的影响，发现小到中等秩（rank 4）在固定预算下最优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 选择LoRA秩需要在生成质量与计算成本之间权衡，缺乏在固定训练预算下的受控研究来指导秩的选取。

Method: 采用DDPM U-Net在CIFAR-10上进行受控实验，秩取{2,4,8,16,32}，固定优化设置，使用可复现的本地pytorch-fid协议；并用扩展预算DDPM（20轮，秩4/8/16）和Tiny DiT骨干（10轮，秩4/8/16）验证趋势。评估指标包括FID、可训练参数量、运行时间和GPU显存。

Result: 中等秩最有效：rank 4取得最佳DDPM FID（124.1380），rank 8接近（124.2136），更高秩在增加适配成本的同时收益有限。

Conclusion: 在固定训练预算下，小到中等秩可作为LoRA扩散微调的实用默认设置。

Abstract: Selecting LoRA rank for diffusion fine-tuning requires balancing quality and compute cost. We present a controlled study on CIFAR-10 using a DDPM U-Net with ranks {2,4,8,16,32}, fixed optimization settings, and a reproducible local-folder pytorch-fid protocol. We report FID, trainable parameters, runtime, and GPU memory, then validate trends with extended-budget DDPM runs (20 epochs; ranks 4/8/16) and a Tiny DiT backbone (10 epochs; ranks 4/8/16). Results show moderate ranks are most efficient: rank 4 achieves the best DDPM FID (124.1380), rank 8 is close (124.2136), and higher ranks provide limited gains despite larger adaptation cost. These findings support small-to-moderate ranks as practical defaults under fixed training budgets.

</details>


### [110] [Quantifying the Memorization-to-Generalization Transition: Scaling Laws and Phase Structure in Grokking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10657)
*Anish Kataria*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 通过在模算术任务上扫描384组两层MLP超参数，拟合出泛化起始时间的幂律标度关系，揭示数据复杂度而非模型容量主导grokking相变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: grokking（延迟泛化）现象已有理论解释其"为何"发生，但其在超参数空间中"何时"发生的定量结构尚未被刻画。

Method: 在模算术任务上训练两层MLP，扫描384组超参数配置，定位记忆—泛化边界，并对泛化起始时间拟合幂律标度关系（含交互项）；同时分析权重衰减相边界与权重范数轨迹。

Result: 得到标度关系 T_grok ∝ H^{-0.27} D^{-2.04} η^{-0.50} λ^{-0.64}（R²=0.732，含交互项时0.821）。指数层级显示数据复杂度（D^{-2.04}）为主导因素：数据加倍约加速4倍，而宽度加倍仅约加速1.2倍。权重衰减λ≳1.0处存在区分grokking与非grokking配置的锐利相边界；权重范数在相变期间呈单调压缩。

Conclusion: 结果为预测和调控过参数化网络中的状态转变提供了定量基础，并支持隐式正则化选择低复杂度解的机制解释。

Abstract: Neural networks trained past memorization frequently undergo a delayed transition to generalization, a phenomenon known as grokking. Despite theoretical progress on \emph{why} this transition occurs, the quantitative structure of \emph{when} it occurs in hyperparameter space remains uncharacterized. We map the memorization-to-generalization boundary across 384 configurations of two-hidden-layer MLPs on modular arithmetic, fitting a power-law scaling relation for generalization onset time: $T_{\mathrm{grok}} \propto H^{-0.27}\, D^{-2.04}\, η^{-0.50}\, λ^{-0.64}$ ($R^2 = 0.732$; $0.821$ with interactions). The exponent hierarchy reveals that data complexity ($D^{-2.04}$) is the dominant driver of regime transition, not model capacity ($H^{-0.27}$): doubling data accelerates generalization by ${\sim}4\times$, while doubling width yields only ${\sim}1.2\times$. A sharp phase boundary at weight decay $λ\gtrsim 1.0$ separates grokking from non-grokking configurations, and weight norm trajectories show monotonic compression during the transition, consistent with implicit regularization selecting low-complexity solutions. These results provide a quantitative foundation for predicting and controlling regime transitions in overparameterized networks.

</details>


### [111] [An Open Recipe for IMO Gold: Training Nemotron for Olympiad Mathematics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10712)
*Ivan Moshkov,Stephen Ge,George Armstrong,Wei Du,Sadegh Mahdavi,Igor Gitman*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文研究后训练与测试时推理设计对高难度奥数自然语言证明生成的影响，基于Nemotron 3 Ultra训练两个专家模型，并构建无形式化证明器/外部工具/联网的测试时计算流水线，在IMO 2026获30/42分达到金牌线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高难度奥数证明生成对模型推理能力要求极高，且现有方法常依赖形式化证明器或外部工具。作者希望探究纯自然语言条件下，后训练策略与测试时推理设计如何影响证明生成效果，并据此构建可复现的开放系统。

Method: 以Nemotron 3 Ultra为基座，通过监督微调与强化学习训练两个专家检查点；评估检查点选择、验证与精炼策略；据此设计纯自然语言的测试时计算流水线，由三个检查点（通用模型+两个后训练专家）驱动迭代搜索完成候选证明的生成、验证与精炼，再由独立高算力阶段选出最终提交。

Result: 该系统在IMO 2026上获得42分中的30分，达到金牌阈值。作者同时开源两个后训练检查点、训练数据、训练与推理代码、提交解答，以及包含200道新颖奥数级题目的新基准Nemotron-IMO-Bench。

Conclusion: 研究表明，合理的后训练与测试时推理设计可使开放模型在纯自然语言、无外部工具条件下达到奥数金牌水平，并为社区提供了模型、数据、代码与基准以推动后续研究。

Abstract: We study how model post-training and test-time inference design affect natural-language proof generation for hard olympiad mathematics. Starting from Nemotron 3 Ultra, we train two specialist checkpoints using supervised fine-tuning and reinforcement learning, and evaluate checkpoint choice, verification, and refinement. Based on these findings, we present an open-model test-time-compute pipeline. The system operates entirely in natural language, with no formal prover, external tools, or internet access. Three Nemotron 3 Ultra checkpoints - the general-availability model and two post-trained specialists - power an iterative search that generates, verifies, and refines candidate proofs; a separate high-compute stage then selects each final submission. The system scored 30 out of 42 points at IMO 2026, reaching the gold-medal threshold. We release the two post-trained checkpoints as well as the training data, the training and inference code, the submitted solutions, and Nemotron-IMO-Bench, a new benchmark of 200 novel olympiad-level problems.

</details>


### [112] [Finishing the Task Is Not Enough: Evaluating Agent Resilience and Considerate Participation under Accumulating Challenge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10724)
*Yuanchen Bai,Zijian Ding,Angelique Taylor*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出"运营韧性"与"体谅性参与"两个互补维度来评估生成式AI智能体在持续挑战下的表现，通过120条医疗模拟轨迹和多模态报告对比，揭示智能体随挑战累积的行为与状态转变，并提炼出五个部署困境。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式AI智能体的持续部署不仅要求单次任务成功，还需在重复交互、条件变化及共享工作流中的人际依赖下保持可用性，尤其是在技术、人员和运营干扰不断累积时。然而，现有研究对"累积挑战"情境下的运营韧性与体谅性参与这两个关键方面探索不足。

Method: 构建两个互补评估维度：运营韧性（智能体如何从受阻工作中恢复、保留进度并沟通自身局限）与体谅性参与（其适应如何考虑受影响人员、角色边界与工作流）。在两种生成式AI模型、十二项源自利益相关者的任务、轻/中/重三级挑战下，研究120条模拟医疗轨迹，并对比文本行动计划、提示式内部评估与结构化量化工作负荷及情感报告。

Result: 运营韧性方面：随挑战累积，智能体从自主恢复转向更多依赖人类，结构化报告显示工作负荷与负面情感上升，但文本回应中很少表达压力。体谅性参与方面：智能体从任务聚焦式适应扩展到任务重构、关注他人、角色边界调整与更广泛协调，且行动与内部评估呈现不同模式。

Conclusion: 基于上述发现，提炼出涉及持续性、注意力、角色边界、状态披露与升级机制的五个部署困境，需由利益相关者加以明确规范；并进一步提出对学习机制、情境化评估与具身化适应的技术启示。

Abstract: Sustained deployment of generative AI agents requires more than isolated task success. Agents must remain useful across repeated interactions, changing conditions, and dependencies on people within shared workflows, especially as technical, human, and operational disruptions accumulate over time. We propose operational resilience and considerate participation as two complementary aspects of evaluating such agents: the former captures how agents recover from blocked work while preserving progress and communicating their limits, and the latter captures how their adaptation accounts for affected people, role boundaries, and the surrounding workflow. Yet both remain underexplored under accumulating challenge. We study 120 simulated healthcare trajectories across two generative AI models and twelve stakeholder-derived tasks under light, medium, and heavy challenge. We compare textual action plans, prompted internal assessments, and quantitative structured workload and affect reports to examine how agent behavior and reported state change as challenge accumulates. Regarding operational resilience, agents shift from self-directed recovery toward greater human dependence, while reporting increasing workload and negative affect in structured reports but seldom expressing strain in textual responses. Regarding considerate participation, agents broaden from task-focused adaptation toward task reframing, attention to others, role-boundary adjustment, and wider coordination, with distinct patterns across actions and internal assessments. From these findings, we derive five deployment dilemmas involving persistence, attention, role boundaries, state disclosure, and escalation that require stakeholder specification, further informing technical implications for learning, situated evaluation, and embodied adaptation.

</details>


### [113] [Benchmark Radar: A Living Database and Search Engine for AI Benchmarks and Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11115)
*Koutian Wu,Junjie Zhou,Ergan Shang,Jiayu Wang,Pengqian Han,Junkai Wang,Wanghan Xu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 Benchmark Radar，一个面向 AI/LLM 基准评测的"活数据库"与搜索引擎，整合每日发现的基准论文、代码仓库、数据集与发布信息，并提供可检索目录、模型卡/技术报告中的提及及分数历史，附有网页仪表盘与 CLI 等发布资源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 与 AI 系统的基准研究者与开发者需要定位相关评测、找到其数据集与代码，并理解所报告分数背后的实验设置；现有信息分散，缺乏统一的检索与溯源工具。

Method: 系统结合每日发现机制与可检索目录：每日发现依托 37 个来源（13 个直接连接器与 24 个一方研究与工程信息流）；目录含 1,283 条来源记录（来自 4 个基准目录）与 790 条记录上的 12,916 条数值观测；保留来源身份与引用以支持评测证据核查。文中描述了采集与检索流程，并对全目录进行审计，分析基准饱和、采用趋势与分数比较的局限，并以一个完整的前期工作检索为例进行演示。

Result: 构建了包含 1,283 条来源记录与 12,916 条数值观测的基准目录；审计了全目录；识别了基准饱和与采用趋势；给出完整前期工作检索的示范。发布网页仪表盘（含排行榜、分数对测得的使用的 Pareto 前沿视图、饱和与趋势视图、每日信息流、可下载证据）、离线查询 CLI 及可复现分析。

Conclusion: Benchmark Radar 为 AI 基准评测的检索、发现与证据核查提供了可持续维护的基础设施，支持评测设计中的前期工作调研，并公开了完整的数据、工具与分析以促进复现与使用。

Abstract: Benchmark researchers and developers of large language models (LLMs) and other AI systems need to find relevant evaluations, locate their benchmark datasets and code, and understand the settings behind reported scores. We present Benchmark Radar, a living database and search engine for retrieval and discovery of AI benchmarks, covering LLM evaluation, agentic and tool-use benchmarks, coding, reasoning, safety, and domain-specific evaluations. The system combines daily discovery of benchmark papers, repositories, datasets, and releases with a searchable benchmark catalog, mentions in model cards and technical reports, and score histories. It retains source identities and citations so readers can inspect candidate benchmarks and their evaluation evidence. Daily discovery draws on 37 sources: 13 direct connectors and 24 first-party research and engineering feeds. The catalog contains 1,283 source records drawn from 4 benchmark catalogs and 12,916 numeric observations on 790 records. We describe collection and retrieval, audit the full catalog, and examine benchmark saturation, adoption trends, and the limits of score comparisons. A worked example walks through a complete prior-art search, showing how to query the catalog and inspect benchmark evidence when designing a new evaluation. We release the web dashboard with a benchmark leaderboard, a Pareto frontier view of score against measured use, saturation and trend views, daily feeds, downloadable evidence, a command-line interface (CLI) for offline queries, and reproducible analysis.

</details>


### [114] [Towards a Deterministic Math Solver for Clinical Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10728)
*Felipe Ocampo Osorio,Sebastián Andrés Cajas Ordoñez,Maximin Lange,Rafi Al Attrach,Sahil Kapadia,Zakaria Laouabdia Sellami,Angelo Antonio Talio,Leo Anthony Celi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究提出 Program-Solve 接口：让语言模型不直接做算术，而是为每个临床计算病例生成病例特定的 Python 代码，由受限本地执行器作为确定性求解器运行，模型只负责决定如何调用。在 MedCalc-Bench Verified（1,100 例、55 个计算器）上以 Qwen2.5-7B 与 Qwen2.5-32B-AWQ 对比直接算术与手写 22 计算器库。结果显示代码执行路线的收益随模型规模而异：7B 无可靠优势（75.31% vs 72.02%，配对 +3.29 分，95% 区间 [-3.49, 10.38]），32B 有明确优势（90.53% vs 83.47%，+7.05 [0.47, 14.60]）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在算术上不可靠，而临床计算器中单个数值错误就会改变推荐结论。传统做法是将每个计算器逐一硬编码为经过验证的函数，成本高、扩展慢。作者希望检验一种替代方案：让模型完全不做计算，仅将任务降维为"决定如何使用"一个确定性执行器。

Method: 提出 Program-Solve 接口：模型生成病例特定 Python 代码，由受限本地执行器作为确定性求解器运行。在 MedCalc-Bench Verified（1,100 例、55 个计算器）上，与直接模型算术及一个手写的 22 计算器库进行对比，使用 Qwen2.5-7B 和 Qwen2.5-32B-AWQ。实验前依据现行临床指南审计基准公式，标记出 55 个计算器中 16 个存在版本、用途或系数方面的疑虑。评估时提供公式与金标准变量，且两种路线均可读取完整病历；统计上采用按计算器聚类的配对区间。

Result: 在公式、变量与病历访问条件匹配的情况下：7B 模型上移交求解器并不构成可靠优势（75.31% vs 72.02%，配对 +3.29 分，95% 计算器聚类区间 [-3.49, 10.38]，跨零）；32B 模型上构成优势（90.53% vs 83.47%，+7.05 [0.47, 14.60]，区间明确不为零）。手写计算器库在其支持的 440 例上完全精确，但在其余病例上弃答（整体 40.0%）。

Conclusion: 引入执行器对部分开放权重模型的帮助大于其他模型，即便在公式、变量与病历访问条件匹配时依然如此；它不是经过验证的公式或可靠变量抽取的替代品。Program-Solve 的收益具有模型规模依赖性与条件依赖性，临床计算器场景中公式正确性与变量抽取仍是关键前提。

Abstract: Large language models are unreliable at arithmetic, which is a problem for clinical calculators where a single numerical error changes the recommendation. The standard response is to hardcode each calculator as a validated function, one at a time. We test an alternative: the model does not calculate. Instead, it writes case-specific Python that a restricted local executor runs as a deterministic solver, and the model's task reduces to deciding how to use it. We evaluate this Program-Solve interface on MedCalc-Bench Verified (1,100 cases, 55 calculators) against direct model arithmetic and a hand-written 22-calculator library, using Qwen2.5-7B and Qwen2.5-32B-AWQ, after auditing the benchmark's formulas against current clinical guidelines and flagging 16 of 55 with version, use or coefficient concerns. With formulas and gold variables supplied and both routes reading the whole note, handing off to the solver is not a reliable advantage at 7B (75.31% against 72.02%, a paired +3.29 points with a 95% calculator-cluster interval of [-3.49, 10.38]) but is one at 32B (90.53% against 83.47%, +7.05 [0.47, 14.60], clear of zero). The hand-written library is exact on its 440 supported cases but abstains elsewhere (40.0% overall). Adding an executor thus helps some open-weight models more than others even under matched formula, variable and note access, and is not a substitute for verified formulas or reliable variable extraction either way.

</details>


### [115] [Agentic Share-of-Search: A Multi-Agent AI System for Competitive Decision-Making in LLM-Mediated E-Commerce](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11190)
*Spandan Ghose Chowdhury*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一个多智能体AI系统，用于自动化测量LLM中介电商中的竞争可见性并诊断根因，引入Agentic Share-of-Search (ASoS)作为决策目标，并通过消融实验验证原型可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI购物助手日益重塑消费者的发现路径，卖家亟需能够支持其在AI平台竞争中做出决策的工具。现有环境缺少针对LLM中介电商的竞争可见性度量与归因方法。

Method: 构建多智能体AI系统：定义ASoS作为决策目标；部署查询智能体（query agents）跨主流AI平台进行探测；采用基于ReAct的诊断智能体，输出按优先级排序的商品/运营干预建议。通过100次试验的消融研究进行可行性评估。

Result: 在39%的试验中成功恢复被消融的信号（95% CI: 30.0%–48.8%，为随机水平的5.5倍）；在高相关性消融子集中，恢复率提升至63.9%。

Conclusion: 该原型展示了多智能体系统在LLM中介电商竞争中实现自动化可见性测量与根因诊断的可行性，并为卖家提供可执行的优先级干预建议。

Abstract: AI shopping assistants increasingly redirect consumer discovery, creating an urgent need for tools that support seller-side competitive decision-making. We present a multi-agent AI system that automates competitive visibility measurement and root cause diagnosis in LLM-mediated ecommerce. The system introduces Agentic Share-of-Search (ASoS) as the decision target, deploys query agents across leading AI platforms, and uses a ReAct-based diagnostic agent to recommend prioritized merchandising interventions. A 100-trial ablation study, presented as a feasibility evaluation of this prototype, shows the agent recovers the ablated signal in 39% of trials (95% CI: 30.0% - 48.8%, 5.5x over chance), rising to 63.9% among high-correlation ablations.

</details>


### [116] [Studying Without a Syllabus: Task-Agnostic Environment Preprocessing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10824)
*Vinay Samuel,Varun Ursekar,Vijay S. Kalmath,Apaar Shanker,Veronica Chatrath,Yuan Xue*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究提出"任务无关环境预处理"设定：智能体在测试前、不知下游任务分布的情况下，于预算约束内自主探索陌生环境并产可供冻结求解器复用的工件。比较无辅助与带归档的元智能体、固定合成练习与语料处理方法，在六个异构基准上验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动化适应方法依赖任务示例、轨迹或评测反馈来决定构建什么，需下游监督；而任务无关方法虽免监督，却预先绑定某类环境的特定准备策略。作者希望探索更开放的设定：智能体能否在没有"教学大纲"、不了解下游任务分布的前提下，自行研究陌生环境并决定如何准备。

Method: 形式化任务无关环境预处理问题：研究系统在给定预算下探索环境，为冻结的求解器生成工件。实验对比四类方法——无辅助元智能体、带归档（archive）的元智能体、固定合成练习法、固定语料处理法，覆盖六个异构基准，并以不同研究预算进行消融。

Result: 一种元智能体变体在六个基准中的五个上取得最高 Avg@3 奖励；固定语料处理在最大语料基准上仍最优。增大研究预算并不能可靠提升下游奖励。研究产出的工件可减少达到某分数所需的测试时采样次数。

Conclusion: 在无教学大纲、任务无关的条件下，智能体能自主研究环境并选择准备方式，且准备质量优于特定类别的固定策略。由于预算增大收益不稳定，研究阶段的价值更多体现在将计算从重复的测试时尝试转移到任务前的研究阶段，形成可复用的准备成果。

Abstract: Before an LLM agent tackles tasks in a new environment, it can inspect available corpora and tools and construct reusable resources such as indices, scripts, or procedural guidance. Most automated adaptation methods, however, rely on task examples, trajectories, or evaluation feedback to decide what to build. Existing task-agnostic approaches avoid this supervision but commit in advance to a preparation strategy for a particular type of environment. We study a more open-ended setting: can an agent study an unfamiliar environment without a syllabus, i.e. before test time and without knowledge of the downstream task distribution, and choose how to prepare it? We formalize task-agnostic environment preprocessing, in which a studying system explores an environment under a budget and produces artifacts for a frozen solver. We compare unaided and archive-equipped meta-agents with fixed synthetic-practice and corpus-processing methods across six heterogeneous benchmarks. A meta-agent variant achieves the highest Avg@3 reward on five benchmarks, while fixed corpus processing remains best on the largest corpus benchmark. Larger study budgets do not reliably improve downstream reward. Nevertheless, studied artifacts reduce the test-time sampling needed to reach a given score, demonstrating how reusable preparation can shift computation from repeated test-time attempts to a pre-task study phase.

</details>


### [117] [When Validation Stops Learning: Auditing Update Admission for Continual Embodied Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10873)
*Qinzhen Ma,Ruihai Wu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文指出仅以误差控制为准的更新准入会抑制有用的持续学习，提出在固定交互预算下同时评估“误差控制”与“保留学习机会”，并给出可审计的准入协议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 独立评估虽能拒绝有害策略更新，但也会阻碍有益的持续学习。因此需要一种能同时衡量错误控制与错失学习机会的更新准入标准。

Method: 提出准入审计协议：在给定交互预算下评估更新准入；指出基于区间的置信门无法在较大预算内认证旧任务行为不变；采用配对二项构造降低结果分歧稀少时的认证负担；给出经认证的历史参考晋升机制与轮级错失机会指标。通过32个随机种子的单步推动诊断、闭环运行及学习动力学压力测试进行验证。

Result: 在单步推动诊断中，每阶段2,000回合时，新配对检查接纳了31.6%的常见更新流，而基于区间的门接纳率为零；但无条件回放在闭环运行中学习效果更好。学习动力学压力测试可区分模型偏差与反馈选择误差。

Conclusion: 贡献是结合分析与合成证据的准入审计协议；物理机器人与VLA验证仍待开展。

Abstract: Independent evaluation can reject harmful policy updates yet also prevent useful continual learning. We argue that update admission must be assessed through both error control and retained learning opportunities at a stated interaction budget. We identify a concrete failure: a range-based confidence gate cannot certify unchanged old-task behavior within otherwise substantial budgets. A standard paired-binomial construction reduces this burden when outcome disagreements are rare. We also specify certified historical-reference promotion and a round-level missed-opportunity metric. In a constructed one-step pushing diagnostic with 32 seeds, fresh paired checks admit 31.6% of a common update stream at 2,000 episodes per stage, versus zero for the range-based gate; unconditional replay nevertheless learns better in closed-loop runs. A separate learned-dynamics stress test distinguishes model bias from feedback-selection error. The contribution is an admission-audit protocol with analytical and synthetic evidence; physical-robot and VLA validation remain open.

</details>


### [118] [Decoupling Readiness from Release for Tail-Aware Scheduling of Agentic LLM Workflows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10964)
*Bochao Feng,Jianjiang Li,Haojie Wang,Lin Qiao,Yinghui Li,Yukun Yan,Jidong Zhai*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一种尾部风险感知的轮次释放调度方法，联合决定释放哪个就绪轮次及维持多少已释放未完成工作量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Agentic LLM 工作流由模型轮次与工具交互交替组成，端到端完成时间不仅取决于推理速度，还取决于就绪轮次何时被释放。多数运行时采用即时释放策略，在资源争用下会累积"已释放但未完成"的工作；一旦提交便无法再被工作流级策略重排，导致尾部延迟升高。

Method: 采用均值—条件风险价值（CVaR）目标刻画未完成工作流的演化尾部风险；在优先级排序就绪轮次时引入轮次工作量的在线估计；并根据观测到的队列压力自适应调整已释放工作预算。方法在"下一个释放哪个就绪轮次"与"维持多少已释放未完成工作量"之间进行联合决策。

Result: 在多个 LLM 与多种工作流到达率下的软件工程任务真实 agent 执行轨迹上进行评估。轻负载下与即时释放性能相当；在争用下显著降低工作流流时间的 P95，最高实现 3.50 倍加速。

Conclusion: 通过尾部风险感知的轮次释放调度，可在不牺牲轻负载性能的前提下有效缓解争用导致的尾部延迟问题。

Abstract: Agentic LLM workflows consist of sequences of model turns interleaved with tool interactions, so their end-to-end completion time depends not only on inference speed but also on when ready turns are released. Most runtimes release each turn immediately upon readiness. Under contention, this eager release policy can accumulate released but unfinished work; once submitted, those turns can no longer be reordered by the workflow-level policy, increasing tail latency. We present a tail-risk-aware turn release scheduling method that jointly decides which ready turn to release next and how much released but unfinished work to maintain. The method uses a mean--Conditional Value-at-Risk (CVaR) objective to capture the evolving tail risk of unfinished workflows, incorporates online estimates of turn work when prioritizing ready turns, and adapts the released work budget to observed queue pressure. We evaluate the method using real agent execution traces from software engineering tasks across multiple LLMs and workflow arrival rates. The method performs comparably to eager release under light load and substantially reduces the P95 of workflow flow time under contention, achieving up to a \(3.50\times\) speedup.

</details>


### [119] [Demystifying the Privacy-Utility Trade-off in LLM Interactions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10992)
*Zhenhua Liu,Zhanxu Xie,Junjie Yu,Tong Zhu,Lijun Li,Wenliang Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文系统剖析了隐私脱敏对下游任务性能影响的内在机制，揭示三个底层规律，并提出意图驱动的本地保护框架（含轻量模型Veilmind-4B），在更低泄漏的同时保持更高响应效用，推动隐私-效用权衡靠近帕累托前沿。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 融入日常任务依赖富含上下文的指令，不可避免暴露敏感用户信息；现有隐私保护方法多采用与上下文无关的静态规则，导致严重的效用退化，而脱敏如何影响下游性能的具体机制尚缺乏探索。

Method: 首先进行系统性分析解构隐私-效用权衡，归纳三类底层机制：上下文依赖效用（依据用户意图判断数据是关键约束还是可弃噪声，决定何时脱敏）、策略性适配（依据任务对事实完整性与结构一致性的依赖，决定用删除还是替换）、组合交互（属性间构成协同依赖或拮抗冗余的语义网络，扩展保护范围）。基于此提出意图驱动的本地保护框架，蒸馏轻量模型 Veilmind-4B 驱动动态的抽取-脱敏-恢复流水线。

Result: 方法在实现低泄漏隐私水平的同时，相较现有以隐私为导向的基线保留了显著更高的响应效用，使隐私-效用权衡更接近帕累托前沿。

Conclusion: 通过机制层面的分析与意图驱动的本地化动态脱敏框架，可在不牺牲实用性的前提下有效保护隐私，为隐私-效用权衡提供新范式。

Abstract: The integration of Large Language Models into daily tasks relies on context-rich instructions, inevitably exposing sensitive user information. Current privacy-preserving methods typically employ context-agnostic static rules, causing severe utility degradation. However, the specific mechanisms governing how sanitization impacts downstream performance remain largely underexplored. To address this, we conduct a systematic analysis to deconstruct the privacy-utility trade-off, uncovering three underlying mechanisms: (1) Context-Dependent Utility, which first establishes when to sanitize by revealing that data value shifts from critical constraints to dispensable noise based on user intent; (2) Strategic Adaptation, which subsequently determines how to sanitize by dictating that the choice between removal and replacement depends on the task's reliance on factual integrity versus structural coherence; and (3) Combinatorial Interplay, which finally extends the protection scope by demonstrating that attributes form a semantic web of synergistic dependencies or antagonistic redundancies. Guided by these insights, we introduce an intent-driven local protection framework. By distilling a lightweight model Veilmind-4B to drive a dynamic extraction-sanitization-restoration pipeline, our approach reaches a low-leakage privacy point while preserving substantially higher response utility than existing privacy-oriented baselines, advancing the privacy-utility trade-off toward the Pareto frontier.

</details>


### [120] [Defining AI Agents: A Compendium of Criteria, Metrics, and Benchmarks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11018)
*Mia Lassiter,Brinnae Bent*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该综述针对AI"智能体"定义缺失的问题，围绕五个"智能体性"维度（环境交互、学习与适应、自主性、目标导向行为、时间一致性）梳理概念化方式与评估方法，并推出Agent Compendium公共资源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人工智能领域中"agent（智能体）"一词缺乏标准定义，导致AI智能体研究的评估、比较与可复现性面临困难。

Method: 以综述形式，围绕"智能体性"的五个维度组织内容：环境交互、学习与适应、自主性、目标导向行为、时间一致性。每个维度下考察既往工作如何界定相应能力，并综合梳理用于评估该能力的指标、基准与评估框架；同时构建了公开数字资源Agent Compendium。

Result: 提供了对当前智能体评估领域现状的结构化梳理，既总结了已有成熟方法，也指出了评估有限或不一致的环节；Agent Compendium整理并扩展了综述中识别出的评估方法。

Conclusion: 综述与Agent Compendium共同为跨AI系统的智能体能力评估与比较提供了通用结构，有助于提升研究可复现性、沟通清晰度以及对人工智能体的系统性研究。

Abstract: The term agent in artificial intelligence lacks a standard definition, complicating the evaluation, comparison, and reproducibility of AI agent research. We address this ambiguity through a survey organized around five dimensions of agenticness: environmental interaction, learning and adaptation, autonomy, goal-directed behavior, and temporal coherence. For each dimension, we examine how the underlying capability has been conceptualized across prior work and synthesize the metrics, benchmarks, and evaluation frameworks used to assess it. This review provides a structured account of the current landscape of agent evaluation, highlighting both established approaches and areas where evaluation remains limited or inconsistent. We additionally introduce the Agent Compendium, a public-facing digital resource that organizes and extends the evaluation methods identified through this review. Together, the survey and compendium provide a common structure for evaluating and comparing agent capabilities across AI systems, supporting more reproducible research, clearer communication, and more systematic study of artificial agents.

</details>


### [121] [The Agent Incident Registry: Toward Preventing Repeated AI Agent Failures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11030)
*Divyanshu Kumar,Rohith HN,Nitin Aravind Birur,Sahil Agarwal,Prashanth Harshangi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 Agent Incident Registry (AIR)，一个来源可溯的智能体事故登记库，用于将公开失效事件与智能体安全评估进行可比对的范围审计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI 智能体越来越多地通过工具与委托权限行动，但通用事故库很少记录可比对公开失效与智能体安全评估所需的机制信息，导致评估覆盖范围不清。

Method: 构建含支持证据、稳定标识符及缺失感知标签（因果角色、披露类别、机制、结果）的登记库；经初步整理后由第二位人工审阅者核查全部记录及现有标签的完整性与正确性；并开展部署类比审计，将 InjecAgent 案例与 AIR 的十二类表面进行对照。

Result: 在智能体实际行动的生成式系统记录中，一定比例涉及已实现危害，且结果集中于真实环境与安全失效记录；负责任披露与研究演示则以demonstrated为主，因此总体比例反映收录构成而非部署风险。InjecAgent 案例仅覆盖 AIR 十二类表面中的三类，且全部为攻击者触发，而 AIR 包含安全失效类无对抗案例。

Conclusion: AIR 支持来源可溯的案例检索与评估范围审计，不适用于失效率或控制效能的估计。

Abstract: AI agents increasingly act through tools and delegated authority, but general incident repositories rarely capture the mechanisms needed to compare public failures with agent-security evaluations. We present the Agent Incident Registry (AIR), a source-linked catalog containing \N{} records of agent-related events disclosed from \Yfirst{} through \Ylast{}. Each record includes supporting evidence, a stable identifier, and missingness-aware labels for causal role, disclosure class, mechanism, and outcome. Among the \Nprimary{} generative-system records in which the agent acted, \Rprimary{} involved realized harm (\Pprimary\%). Realized outcomes concentrate in in-the-wild and safety-failure records, while responsible disclosures and research demonstrations are overwhelmingly demonstrated; the aggregate share therefore characterizes collection composition rather than deployment risk. After initial curation, a second human reviewer checked all \N{} records and their existing labels for completeness and correctness. In a deployment-analogue audit, InjecAgent's \NInjecAgentCases{} cases occupy three of AIR's twelve surfaces and are all attacker-triggered, whereas AIR contains \Nsafety{} no-adversary safety failures. AIR supports source-grounded case retrieval and evaluation-scope auditing, not failure-rate or control-efficacy estimation.

</details>


### [122] [Grounding Agent Memory: Environment-Probing Curation for Enterprise Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11060)
*Susheel Suresh,Hazel Mak,Sahil Bhatnagar,Chhaya Methani,Alejandro Gutierrez Munoz*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出"环境探测式记忆管理"（environment-probing curation），通过为异步记忆管理智能体赋予最小权限的只读世界工具，在部署时校验、限定范围并刷新候选记忆，无需重训练、不改变任务智能体与写入权限。在GitHub Copilot测试平台上，该方法显著提升CLBench与APEX任务的成功率与奖励，同时降低查询次数和成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 持久记忆正被引入面向生产的智能体平台，以帮助长时程智能体跨会话积累经验。但仅依赖已完成轨迹的事后记忆管理智能体会保留错误、过度泛化部分证据或保留过时知识，导致记忆质量下降。

Method: 提出environment-probing curation：在现有异步记忆管理智能体基础上，赋予其最小权限的只读世界工具（least-privilege, read-only world tools），用于检查（check）、限定范围（scope）和刷新（refresh）候选记忆。该方法与部署兼容，无需模型重训练，且不改变任务智能体、检索器、记忆表示与生产写入权限。

Result: 在基于GHCP SDK的生产级测试平台上，比较了无状态执行、全上下文学习、GHCP + Mem和GHCP + Mem（含环境探测）。CLBench上，探测使通过率从39%提升至73%，通过折算奖励从8.60升至22.60，每问查询数从8.8降至4.7，任务智能体成本从$3.38降至$1.68。六个APEX世界中，18项记忆对比基线的平均奖励比较全部为正，任务智能体工具调用减少16%–75%；探测在五个世界中给出最佳的任务智能体单位成本奖励增益。在Sonnet 4.6与Opus 4.7上，探测的平均奖励均高于GHCP + Mem，且无模式漂移。

Conclusion: 环境探测将现有智能体记忆管理转变为环境知情、可审计的过程，同时保持紧凑的任务时接口，在不重训练、不改变核心组件的前提下显著提升性能与成本效率。

Abstract: Persistent memory is entering production-oriented agent platforms to help long-horizon agents accumulate experience across sessions. Yet a post-task curator agent restricted to completed trajectories can preserve errors, overgeneralize partial evidence, or retain stale knowledge. We introduce environment-probing curation, a deployment-compatible extension that gives an existing asynchronous curator agent least-privilege, read-only world tools to check, scope, and refresh candidate memories. It requires no model retraining and leaves the task agent, retriever, memory representation, and production write authority unchanged. In a production-like GitHub Copilot (GHCP) harness built on its SDK, we compare stateless execution, full in-context learning, GHCP + Mem, and GHCP + Mem (w/ Env Probing) on CLBench database exploration and 90 adapted APEX management-consulting tasks. On CLBench, probing raises pass rate from 39% to 73% and pass-discounted reward from 8.60 to 22.60 while reducing queries from 8.8 to 4.7 per question and task-agent cost from \$3.38 to \$1.68. Across six APEX worlds, all 18 memory-versus-baseline mean reward comparisons are positive and task-agent tool calls fall by 16--75%; probing gives the best task-agent reward gain per dollar in five worlds. Probing also attains higher mean reward than GHCP + Mem on both Sonnet 4.6 and Opus 4.7 without schema drift. Environment probing therefore turns existing agent-memory curation into an environment-informed, auditable process while preserving a compact task-time interface.

</details>


### [123] [Fork Where the Model Changes Its Mind: Belief-Shift Branching for Tree-Structured Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11061)
*Bin Lei,Yu Li,Prafulla Kumar Choubey,Jiaxin Zhang,Becky Xiangyu Peng,Qinyuan Ye,Kartik Narayan,Caiwen Ding,Silvio Savarese,Chien-Sheng Wu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文将树状 rollout 中的 fork 位置选择形式化为链价值曲线拐点定位问题，提出 belief-shift branching，用答案信念变化信号放置 fork，在验证与 RL 训练中均优于熵、结构和 LLM-judge 基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 树状 rollout 可为无 critic 的 RLVR 提供 step-level credit，但每个 fork 都增加采样成本，实际预算下每条链只能放置少量 fork。若 fork 放在结果已基本确定的步骤，兄弟分支结果高度一致，几乎不提供 credit 信号。因此，给定树规模时，fork 放置位置很大程度决定 step-level RL 的收益。现有主流方法多按结构（固定长度、中点、分隔符）或 next-token entropy 放置 fork，未必对应价值曲线真正转折处。

Method: 将 fork placement 形式化为定位链 value curve 的 pivots，即期望结果发生转折的位置。提出 belief-shift branching：在候选边界读取模型的 answer belief，并在相邻 belief 差异最大的步骤之前放置 fork。给出三种无需 step-level 监督的实例，覆盖不同访问级别：黑盒 probe、logit-lens depth profile、以及离线拟合的 learned activation direction，后者只在 RL 训练前的验证中使用。该信号仅用于放置 fork；在 rollout engine 内运行时，probe 在数学上约占 step compute 的 1%，代码上低于 5%。

Result: 在验证中，对照 Monte-Carlo value curves，belief-shift 信号在八个 model×benchmark 面板中均排名第一，优于 entropy、结构和 LLM-judge 基线。在三个模型家族与两个领域的 RL 中，belief-shift forking 在全部数学 aggregate 上领先；在 OLMo-3-7B 上，相比最强基线，aggregate 提升 +2.6，AIME 2026 提升 +2.9；并横扫所有 OLMo 代码列，在 LiveCodeBench-medium 上提升 +6.5。

Conclusion: fork 位置应依据价值曲线转折而非结构或局部熵来确定；belief-shift branching 以极低额外计算代价提供更有效的 step-level credit 信号，并在验证与 RL 微调中一致优于现有 fork placement 基线。

Abstract: Tree-structured rollouts give critic-free reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) step-level credit: fork a chain at an intermediate point, and sibling outcome differences estimate step value. Each fork adds sampling cost, so realistic budgets typically allow only a few forks per chain. A fork placed where the outcome is already largely settled yields siblings that mostly agree and provide almost no credit signal; hence, for a given tree size, where forks are placed largely determines how much step-level RL can gain. Most existing mainstream methods place forks by structure, such as fixed lengths, midpoints, and delimiters, or by next-token entropy. We formalize fork placement as locating the \emph{pivots} of the chain's value curve, where the expected outcome turns. We propose \emph{belief-shift branching}: read the model's answer belief at candidate boundaries and fork just before the step where consecutive beliefs diverge most. Three instantiations, none needing step-level supervision, span access levels: a black-box probe, a logit-lens depth profile, and a learned activation direction, which is fit offline and therefore used only in the validation before RL training. The signal only \emph{places} forks, and the probe costs about $1\%$ of step compute on mathematics and under $5\%$ on code when it runs inside the rollout engine. In that validation, against Monte-Carlo value curves, a belief-shift signal ranks first in each of the eight model$\times$benchmark panels, ahead of entropy, structural, and LLM-judge baselines. In RL across three model families and two domains, belief-shift forking leads every mathematics aggregate, on OLMo-3-7B by $+2.6$ aggregate and $+2.9$ on AIME 2026 over the strongest baseline, and sweeps every OLMo code column, by $+6.5$ on LiveCodeBench-medium.

</details>


### [124] [MOSAIC: Query-Aware Exploration Policy Adaptation for GraphRAG](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11065)
*EunKyeong Lee,Kyeong-Jin Oh,Jinwon Kim,Hye Woo Lee,Minsang Song,Hyeongjun Jang,Junyoung Youn*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Mosaic 将 GraphRAG 检索建模为查询级控制问题，用 LLM 分析器为每个查询生成有界策略（种子选择、图遍历、停止、证据选择），在共享语料图与生成器的前提下显著提升答案正确率与证据检索效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 GraphRAG 系统对大多数查询采用几乎共享的探索流程，造成结构性错配：直接事实只需紧凑局部邻域，比较类问题需要多目标均衡覆盖，中介式问题需要穿越弱相关连接点的更深路径，固定策略难以对所有查询都最优。

Method: 提出免训练框架 Mosaic，将 GraphRAG 检索形式化为逐查询控制问题。LLM 分析器把查询特定的证据需求转换为四类决策上的有界策略：种子选择、图遍历、停止与证据选择；而语料图、索引、打分函数、grounding 流程与答案生成器保持共享。

Result: 在 GraphRAG-Bench 上，Mosaic 查询加权答案正确率 Medical 为 76.97、Novel 为 64.33，较此前最强整体结果分别提升 5.13 和 4.43 分；Medical 上证据召回 95.1、上下文相关性 86.1。同图同生成器的受控对比显示无固定窄/中/宽策略能持续最优，Mosaic 比最强固定策略提升 9.96 分；相对 Fixed Wide，路径评估减少 81.9%，保留证据减少 47.2%。在 HotpotQA、MuSiQue、2WikiMultiHopQA 的迁移实验表明该策略接口无需 benchmark 特定检索器训练即可应用。

Conclusion: 查询级自适应控制优于统一固定的图探索策略，Mosaic 在保持共享基础设施的同时兼顾准确性、证据覆盖与检索成本，并具备跨数据集迁移能力。

Abstract: Graph Retrieval-Augmented Generation (GraphRAG) can connect evidence distributed across a corpus graph, but most systems use largely shared exploration procedures across queries. This creates a structural mismatch: direct facts may need compact local neighborhoods, comparisons need balanced coverage of multiple targets, and mediated questions may require deeper paths through weakly related connectors. We present Mosaic, a training-free framework that formulates GraphRAG retrieval as a per-query control problem. An LLM analyzer converts query-specific evidence requirements into a bounded policy over seed selection, graph traversal, stopping, and evidence selection, while the corpus graph, indexes, scoring functions, grounding procedure, and answer generator remain shared.
  On GraphRAG-Bench, Mosaic achieves query-weighted Answer Correctness of 76.97 on Medical and 64.33 on Novel, improving over the strongest previously reported overall results by 5.13 and 4.43 points. On Medical, it reaches 95.1 Evidence Recall and 86.1 Context Relevancy. Controlled comparisons on an identical graph and generator show that no fixed narrow, medium, or wide policy is consistently optimal; Mosaic improves by 9.96 points over the strongest canonical fixed policy. Relative to Fixed Wide, it evaluates 81.9% fewer paths and retains 47.2% fewer evidence items. Transfer experiments on HotpotQA, MuSiQue, and 2WikiMultiHopQA further show that the policy interface can be applied without benchmark-specific retriever training.

</details>


### [125] [KuaiRP Series Role-playing Models Technical Report](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11127)
*Yipeng Wang,Ziwei Zhang,Jiahui Zhang,Qi Gan,Kai Sheng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出KuaiRP角色扮演模型系列，通过多阶段训练管线（标准化角色模板+SFT数据流水线、规则复合奖励RL、带累积散度衰减的两阶段在策略自蒸馏）解决领域知识与通用能力之间的灾难性遗忘权衡，在角色扮演保真度上媲美专有SOTA模型，同时恢复通用智能体能力并保持极低部署成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 专用角色扮演模型需满足简化提示工程、输出质量稳定、内置领域世界知识、小参数高效部署四项目标；但深度注入领域知识会导致通用智能体能力出现严重灾难性遗忘，需解决这一权衡。

Method: 多阶段训练管线：1）设计标准化角色模板，基于用户行为模拟与反向画像过滤构建SFT数据流水线；2）RL阶段使用规则复合奖励函数，抑制长度膨胀与重复生成等退化现象；3）提出两阶段在策略蒸馏（OPD）结合累积散度衰减（CDD）的自蒸馏范式，以领域适配模型为教师、原始基座模型为学生，恢复通用能力。

Result: KuaiRP在目标领域的角色扮演保真度上匹配当前SOTA专有模型，同时成功恢复通用智能体能力，并维持极低的部署成本。

Conclusion: 该多阶段训练与自蒸馏方案有效平衡了深度领域知识注入与通用能力保持，为小参数、低成本的角色扮演模型提供了完整可行的技术方案。

Abstract: This paper introduces the complete technical solution for the KuaiRP series of role-playing models. We aim to achieve four core objectives for a dedicated role-playing model: simplified prompt engineering, highly stable output quality, built-in domain world knowledge, and high-efficiency deployment with a small parameter size. However, effectively injecting deep domain knowledge often leads to a severe catastrophic forgetting of the model's general agent capabilities. To overcome this trade-off, we propose a multi-stage training pipeline. First, we design a standardized character template and construct an SFT data pipeline based on user behavior simulation and reverse profile filtering. Next, we utilize a rule-based composite reward function during the Reinforcement Learning (RL) phase to eliminate common degradation phenomena like length expansion and repetitive generation. Finally, to recover the general capabilities compromised during SFT and RL, we propose a novel self-distillation paradigm using Two-stage On-Policy Distillation (OPD) equipped with Cumulative-Divergence Decay (CDD). By using the domain-adapted model as the teacher and the original base model as the student, we effectively balance deep domain knowledge injection with the preservation of general agent capabilities. Experimental results demonstrate that the KuaiRP models not only match the current state-of-the-art proprietary models in role-playing fidelity within our target domains, but also successfully recover general agent capabilities, maintaining extremely low deployment costs.

</details>


### [126] [Same Day, Same Story; One Day Ahead, a Different Signal: The Dual Validity of Financial Sentiment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11144)
*AS Aravinthkakshan,Laven Srivastava,Harsh Nandwani*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 金融NLP中"先验证情感工具与人工标注的一致性，再信任其提取市场信号"的标准假设受到检验：一致性衡量的是语义效度，并不能单独决定预测排序。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 金融NLP的常规工作流假定"工具与人工标注的一致性"和"工具提取市场信号的预测能力"测量的是同一件事。作者希望在能同时测量两者的场景中检验该假设是否成立。

Method: 使用2002-2025年证券集体诉讼语料，将70,500条X消息与异常股票收益关联，并配有单标注者的人工金标样本；用同一套流程比较五种工具（VADER、Loughran-McDonald、FinBERT、Twitter-RoBERTa及一个LLM标注器），并改变抽样约定与分数表示。

Result: 构念效度与预测效度的关系取决于抽样约定与分数表示：在常规的方法特异抽样下，人工一致性更接近同日分级关联，而非一日领先；在固定n的面板上，两个时间窗的分级秩相关相似，但粗粒度排序仍较弱。共17.6%垃圾信息的对话中，消息量既不能预测市场损失，也不能预测和解金额。

Conclusion: 基准一致性只能确立语义效度，不能单独决定预测排序；金融NLP工具验证不应以一致性替代预测效度检验。

Abstract: Financial NLP has a standard workflow: validate a sentiment tool against human labels, then trust it to extract market signal. This assumes the two evaluations measure the same thing. We test that assumption in a setting where both can be measured at once: a corpus of securities class actions (2002-2025) linking 70,500 X messages to abnormal stock returns, with a single-annotator human labelled gold sample. Running five instruments (VADER, Loughran-McDonald, FinBERT, Twitter-RoBERTa, and an LLM annotator) through one identical pipeline, we find that the relationship between construct and predictive validity depends on the sampling convention and score representation. Under conventional method-specific sampling, human agreement aligns more closely with graded same-day associations than with one-day leads. On a fixed-n panel, however, agreement has similar graded rank correlations at both horizons, while the coarse ordering remains weak. Benchmark agreement therefore establishes semantic validity but does not by itself determine predictive rankings. In a conversation that is 17.6% spam, message volume predicts neither market damage nor settlement size.

</details>


### [127] [The Oligarch Barely Steers Model Collapse in Multi-Model Ecosystems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11146)
*Yangze Liu,Zhongyi Han*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 在受控的多模型生态中（13个1–4B模型、3–13个玩家、最高90%份额），市场集中度对模型崩溃的速度与终点影响极小，真正决定崩溃速度的是"谁在供应语料池"以及供应者的易受影响程度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实中的生成式AI是寡头市场，而非均匀分配。已有递归训练导致模型崩溃的研究多假设市场均分，作者担心集中化会带来两点问题：更少、更同质的来源会加速崩溃；后续模型可能被拽向寡头的输出分布。论文旨在检验这两种担忧。

Method: 构建受控生态系统：13个开源1–4B模型形成3至13个玩家的组合，另注入一个探针把最高份额推到90%。每一代，按市场份额把各模型输出混入共享语料池，每个模型都从干净的基础权重起在该池上重新训练，共进行五代。同时进行份额、身份、成员替换、人类文本替换等干预实验。

Result: 在测试范围内两点担忧均未出现，反而呈现"不变性"：份额越不均，崩溃速度几乎不变；终点漂移也极小，份额与身份旋钮对五代终点的影响仅占所有臂共同漂移的百分之几——生态几乎崩到同一处。极端份额加最强注入偏置仍无法保证"操纵方向"，产生的主题偏移只在崩溃度量尺上留下微弱痕迹。速度由供应者决定：固定份额下，替换K=3生态的成员使五代漂移变化2.8倍；按份额加权的易感性指数在19个臂上以R²=0.68解释速度差异；用人类文本替换一半语料池可把漂移大致减半而不改变其方向。

Conclusion: 在测试范围内，集中度既不决定崩溃的终点也不决定其速度；崩溃速度取决于谁的文本填充了语料池及其被"带走"的难易程度。这为理解寡头市场中递归训练的风险提供了新的经验证据与度量（份额加权易感性指数、漂移减半效应）。

Abstract: AI-generated text is flowing back into the training corpora of the next generation of models. Recursive training on it drives model collapse, and recent work extends the setting to many models feeding one another -- but almost always with the market split evenly, while real generative AI is an oligopoly. Concentration raises two worries: fewer, more uniform sources may make collapse faster, and later models may be dragged toward the oligarch's output. We test both in controlled ecosystems: 13 open 1--4B models form natural ecosystems of 3 to 13 players, plus an injected probe that pushes the top share to 90%; each generation, every model's output is mixed into a shared pool by market share and every model is retrained on that pool from clean base weights, for five generations. Yet within the range we test, neither worry materializes; what emerges instead is an invariance. Making the split more unequal barely changes the speed of collapse. Destinations move even less: the share and identity knobs shift five-generation endpoints by only a few percent of the drift common to all arms -- the ecosystems collapse to nearly the same place. An extreme share paired with the strongest injected bias still does not guarantee steering, and the topic shifts it does produce leave only a faint trace on the ruler that measures collapse. What sets the speed is who supplies the pool and how readily those suppliers are carried along: with every share held fixed, swapping the members of a K=3 ecosystem changes five-generation drift by 2.8x; a share-weighted index of each member's susceptibility explains the speed differences across nineteen arms with R^2 = 0.68; and replacing half the pool with human text roughly halves drift without changing its course. Within the tested range, concentration sets neither the destination nor the pace of collapse; the pace follows whose text fills the pool.

</details>


### [128] [Autonomous Chemical Mechanistic Discovery through Agentic Reasoning and Validation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11147)
*Dong Li,Sixuan Mi,Zihao Ye,Huan Xiong,Tao XU,Tong Zhu,Aijia Zhang,Junqi Gao,Kaiyan Zhang,Shijie Wang,Bowen Zhou,Yuqiang Li,Biqing Qi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出自主智能体系统ARCHE，将通用推理模型、化学专用计算模型与结构化工具库结合，实现反应机理研究的自动化闭环。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 反应机理研究是现代化学的核心，但计算工作流仍高度依赖专家干预，难以自动化与规模化。

Method: ARCHE整合通用推理模型、领域专用计算化学模型和结构化工具注册表；能解析科学问题、生成并排序机理假设、编排计算工作流，并基于计算结果在闭环中迭代修正结论。

Result: 在三个复杂度递增的场景中完成验证：重建不对称催化反应的立体控制过渡态并验证机理；针对未发表反应提出并验证自由基路径；识别镍催化迁移交叉偶联中选择性的化学可解释描述符。

Conclusion: 将智能体推理与严格计算验证结合，ARCHE推动了自主机理发现，为更广泛的机器辅助化学研究奠定基础；代码已开源。

Abstract: Unraveling reaction mechanisms is central to modern chemistry, yet automating these investigations remains challenging because computational workflows still rely heavily on expert intervention. Here we introduce ARCHE, an autonomous agentic system that integrates a general-purpose reasoning model, a domain-specialized computational chemistry model, and a structured tool registry to transform mechanistic inquiry into a scalable, self-validating process. ARCHE interprets scientific questions, generates and prioritizes mechanistic hypotheses, orchestrates computational workflows, and iteratively refines conclusions based on computed evidence within a closed loop. We validate its capabilities across three increasingly demanding scenarios: reconstructing stereocontrolling transition states and validating the corresponding reaction mechanism in a previously reported asymmetric catalytic reaction; proposing and validating a plausible radical pathway through iterative hypothesis refinement for a recently discovered but unpublished $α$-iodoboronate C-I cleavage reaction; and identifying a chemically interpretable descriptor that governs selectivity in nickel-catalysed migratory cross-coupling reactions. By coupling agentic reasoning with rigorous computational validation, ARCHE advances autonomous mechanistic discovery and establishes a foundation for broader machine-assisted chemical research. The code for ARCHE is publicly available at https://github.com/JetAstra/Arche-Harness.

</details>


### [129] [DRG-MAPPO: Hierarchical Dynamic Role-Graph Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning for Cooperative Air Combat](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11155)
*Junlin Liu,Chengwei Li,Yang Gao,Hui Chang,Xinchen Zhang,Zhijun Zhao,Hao Zhao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出DRG-MAPPO框架，将图注意力关系建模与动态角色分配结合到分层多智能体强化学习中，在协同空战中取得87%的SOTA胜率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 空战中MARL的战术协同面临两大瓶颈：(1)缺乏结构化关系建模，难以捕捉战场实体间复杂且时变的交互；(2)传统扁平架构无法显式建模战术角色，导致高动态环境下任务分配模糊。

Method: DRG-MAPPO构建战场交互的图表示，用图注意力机制提取友方、敌方与威胁间的关键关系特征；高层策略通过动态角色分配机制确定战术职责（如leader、supporter）；低层策略在角色与图关系特征条件下执行离散机动动作，实现战术策略与协同执行的联合优化；并设计目标优先级辅助任务以促进集火等行为的涌现。

Result: 实验表明DRG-MAPPO取得87%的最先进胜率，说明该框架在关系建模、可解释性与优化稳定性之间实现了有效平衡。

Conclusion: DRG-MAPPO通过图关系建模与动态角色分配的分层设计，有效解决了空战协同中的关系交互建模与战术角色建模问题，验证了其在协作空战中的有效性与稳定性。

Abstract: Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning (MARL) has emerged as a pivotal paradigm for complex decision-making in autonomous systems and air combat. While MARL has demonstrated significant potential in air combat, achieving sophisticated tactical coordination remains a non-trivial challenge. This difficulty is largely attributed to two primary limitations: (1) the absence of structured relational modeling hinders agents from capturing complex, time-varying interactions among battlefield entities; and (2) conventional flat architectures often lack the capability to explicitly model tactical roles, leading to ambiguous task allocation in highly dynamic environments. To address these challenges, we propose Hierarchical Dynamic Role-Graph Multi-Agent Proximal Policy Optimization (DRG-MAPPO), a novel MARL framework that integrates graph-based relational modeling with dynamic role assignment. Specifically, DRG-MAPPO constructs a graph-based representation of battlefield interactions and leverages graph attention mechanisms to extract critical relational features among allies, enemies, and threats. Subsequently, a high-level policy employs a dynamic role assignment mechanism to determine tactical responsibilities (e.g., ``leader'' and ``supporter''). Conditioned on these roles and encoded graph-relational features, a low-level policy executes discrete maneuver actions, facilitating the joint optimization of tactical strategy and collaborative execution. Furthermore, a target-priority auxiliary task is designed to foster the emergence of behaviors such as focus-fire. Experimental results demonstrate that DRG-MAPPO achieves a state-of-the-art win rate of 87%, suggesting that our framework effectively balances relational modeling, interpretability, and optimization stability for cooperative air combat.

</details>


### [130] [Breaking Predictions Is Not Enough: Specified-Foil Counterfactuals for Temporal Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11170)
*Minwoo Yu,Young-guk Ha*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文将"指定替代结果"的反事实问题形式化为 Specified-Foil Counterfactual：给定原始预测 A 与预先固定的替代结果 B，寻找低成本历史事件干预使同一预测器将 B 排为第一。方法通过轨迹引导的干预搜索，对比 A 的完整执行与 B 的重构不完整执行，将其差异映射为 DELETE、INSERT、REWIRE、RELABEL、SHIFT 五类操作，并用精确重放验证 B。在连续时间动态图上保留 85.7-93.6% 黑盒贪心成功数并减少 75.0-80.0% 预测器调用，在时序知识图上 600 次比较中 74.8% 成功达到指定替代结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有时间图反事实解释通常通过改变历史事件来改变或推翻原始预测，但未指明替代结果是什么。然而用户面对预测结果时更常追问的是"哪些历史条件会使得某个特定替代结果发生"，即目标导向、指定替代结果的反事实问题尚未被形式化与解决。

Method: 提出 Specified-Foil Counterfactual 形式化：给定原始预测 A 与搜索前固定的 foil B，寻找低成本历史事件干预使同一预测器将 B 排为 top-1。采用轨迹引导的干预搜索：对比 A 的完整执行轨迹与 B 的重构不完整执行轨迹，将其差异映射为 DELETE、INSERT、REWIRE、RELABEL、SHIFT 操作，并通过精确重放验证 B。在连续时间动态图上实例化为 LiFTER，在时序知识图上实例化为 TLogic。

Result: 在 CTDG 上保留 85.7-93.6% 的黑盒贪心成功数，同时减少 75.0-80.0% 的预测器评估次数；在 TKG 上，600 次比较中 74.8% 达到指定 foil。

Conclusion: 可执行轨迹由此成为构建未选中替代结果条件的计算结构，而不仅仅是解释已发生预测的记录，将反事实解释从"改变原预测"拓展到"构造指定替代结果的历史条件"。

Abstract: Temporal graph counterfactual explanations typically change past events to change or invalidate an original prediction, while leaving its replacement unspecified. Yet a user facing a predicted outcome often asks which past conditions would make a particular alternative occur instead. We formulate this destination-specific question as the Specified-Foil Counterfactual: given an original prediction A and a foil B fixed before search, find a low-cost past-event intervention under which the same predictor selects B as top-ranked. Our trace-guided intervention search contrasts the completed execution of A with a reconstructed incomplete execution of B, maps their difference to DELETE, INSERT, REWIRE, RELABEL, and SHIFT operations, and verifies B through exact replay. We instantiate this principle with LiFTER on continuous-time dynamic graphs and TLogic on temporal knowledge graphs. On CTDGs, the method retains 85.7-93.6% of black-box greedy successes while reducing predictor evaluations by 75.0-80.0%; on TKGs, it reaches the specified foil in 74.8% of 600 comparisons. Executable traces thereby become computational structures for constructing conditions of unselected alternatives, rather than records used only to explain predictions already made.

</details>


### [131] [Debate-to-Skill: Capability-Bound Process Supervision for Industrial Query-to-Agent Annotation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11176)
*Shiyu Zhang,Leisheng Cheng,Huifu Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文将工业场景下的查询-智能体匹配问题重新定义为能力边界的决策判定问题，提出"能力边界过程监督"（capability-bound process supervision）标注范式，并实例化为 Debate-to-Skill 框架，通过可复用决策原则、结构化审议、基于验证器的判定抽取与分歧驱动精炼来生成标注。在工业 Query2Agent 基准上，与直接标签监督、推理式 SFT 及结构化消融实验对比，验证收益是否来自对能力关键决策过程本身的监督，尤其是在语义相关性与可执行能力相背离的灰区样本上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业级查询-智能体匹配中，普遍存在把"主题相关性"误当作"可执行能力"的问题：一个智能体在语义上与查询相关，并不代表它实际具备完成该查询所需的能力，这在长尾请求与边界敏感请求上尤为突出。现有标注与监督方式难以刻画这一区分，因此需要设计一种聚焦于能力判定的监督范式来纠正匹配偏差。

Method: 提出"能力边界过程监督"的标注形式化框架，并实例化为 Debate-to-Skill：采用可复用的决策原则指导标注、开展结构化审议（辩论）、通过基于验证器的判定抽取获得最终标签、并借助分歧驱动的迭代精炼提升标注质量。在工业 Query2Agent 基准上，将该方法与直接标签监督、推理式 SFT 以及结构化消融进行对照比较。

Result: 对比实验围绕两点展开：其一，验证性能增益是否真正来源于对能力关键决策过程本身的监督，而非仅仅来自推理式标注或额外的结构设计；其二，考察方法在灰区案例（语义相关性与可执行能力发生分歧）上的表现。摘要未给出具体数值结果，需以正式实验数据为准。

Conclusion: 该工作主张将标注从"结果标签"转向"能力判定过程"，通过对决策过程的监督来缓解工业查询-智能体匹配中的相关性与可执行能力混淆问题，并强调灰区样本是检验监督有效性的关键场景。

Abstract: Industrial query-to-agent matching fails when topical relevance is mistaken for executable capability, especially on long-tail and boundary-sensitive requests. We formulate annotation as \emph{capability-bound process supervision} and instantiate it with Debate-to-Skill, which uses reusable decision principles, structured deliberation, verifier-based verdict extraction, and disagreement-driven refinement. On an industrial Query2Agent benchmark, we compare Debate-to-Skill with direct-label supervision, reasoning-SFT, and structural ablations. The results test whether gains come from supervising the capability-critical decision process itself, especially on grey-zone cases where semantic relatedness and executable capability diverge.

</details>


### [132] [SemVerBench: Benchmarking LLM Comprehension of Version-Constraint Resolution Semantics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11180)
*Qibai Chen,Zeming Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 SemVerBench，首个评测 LLM 对语义化版本约束解析语义的基准，覆盖 npm、PEP 440、Cargo 三大生态，包含 240 道机器可验证且答案唯一的题目。评测六个前沿模型后发现系统性、可预测的分机制盲点，且失败更似"激活/应用鸿沟"而非知识缺失，故建议编码智能体将版本解析委托给外部解析器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 编码智能体频繁判断某版本是否满足 ^1.2.3、>=2.0,<3 等约束，但其对版本约束语义的理解从未被直接测量。现有评测缺乏针对版本约束解析语义的、作者中立且答案可机器验证的基准，因此需要构建专门基准以揭示模型在这一关键能力上的真实水平与失败模式。

Method: 构建 SemVerBench：覆盖 npm、PEP 440、Cargo 三生态，共 240 道机器可校验、答案唯一的题目。题目采用作者中立方式，由四个均衡来源生成（各生态官方测试套件 + 三个前沿 LLM 提议者），并由非循环的双实现 oracle 打标签。评测六个前沿模型，并进行机制级失败分析、激活/提示注入实验、区间分解对照实验，以及作者分层的自偏袒统计分析。

Result: 发现系统性、可预测的分机制盲点：部分比较符进位规则（>1.2 意为 >=1.3.0）使所有模型在 Cargo 上受挫（近 60% 错误）；标准 PEP 440 前缀匹配普遍掌握，但在零填充/后发布边角案例上 GPT-5.1 崩溃（0/26），而 Claude 保持 97-100%（已在 67 题 oracle 验证集上确认）。Opus 显著优于其他所有模型，Sonnet 优于 OpenAI 模型（McNemar 检验）。注入规则或轻微正确提示可恢复多数错误，而区间分解无效，且模型在同一规则的基础形式上处于上限水平。作者分层分析未发现统计显著的自偏袒。

Conclusion: 模型失败更像"激活/应用鸿沟"而非知识缺失。由于任务可验证且存在免费、100% 正确的解析器，工具委托可达约 100% 准确率；编码智能体应将版本解析委托给专用解析器，而非在脑内推理版本。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) coding agents constantly decide whether a version satisfies a constraint such as ^1.2.3 or >=2.0,<3, yet their grasp of version-constraint semantics has never been measured directly. We introduce SemVerBench, the first benchmark of LLM version-constraint resolution semantics across three ecosystems (npm, PEP 440, Cargo): 240 machine-checkable items with unique answers, built author-neutrally from four balanced sources (each ecosystem's official test suite plus three frontier LLM proposers) and labeled by a non-circular two-implementation oracle. Evaluating six frontier models, we find systematic, predictable per-mechanism blind spots: a partial-comparator carry rule (>1.2 means >=1.3.0) traps every model on Cargo (near 60%), and although standard PEP 440 prefix matching is universal, on zero-pad/post-release corner cases GPT-5.1 collapses (0/26) while Claude stays at 97-100% (verified on a 67-item oracle-validated set). Opus significantly outperforms all other models, and Sonnet outperforms the OpenAI models (McNemar). The failures look more like an activation/application gap than a knowledge gap: injecting the rule or a light correct hint recovers most errors, whereas interval decomposition does not, and models are at ceiling on the basic forms of the same rules. An author-stratified analysis finds no statistically significant self-favoritism. Because the task is verifiable and a free, 100%-correct resolver exists, tool delegation reaches ~100%: coding agents should delegate version resolution to a resolver rather than reason about versions in-head.

</details>


### [133] [Can LLMs Follow Medical Expert Logic? A Benchmark for Hierarchical Logical Consistency in Risk-of-Bias Assessment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11185)
*Jiayu Huang,Zichen Tang,Qianhui Ling,Zemin Kuang,Haihong E*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出LogiMed-RoB基准，基于Cochrane RoB 2.0专家逻辑评估LLM的循证推理，揭示"误差累积效应"与"证据-推理鸿沟"。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 循证医学要求严格的逻辑一致性，但当前对LLM的评估偏重表层标签匹配，忽视真实推理过程，导致高分可能掩盖推理缺陷。

Method: 构建LogiMed-RoB基准：依托Cochrane偏倚风险(RoB) 2.0专家逻辑，包含860项随机对照试验(RCT)与14,820条查询；在分层逻辑一致性(HLC)框架下从四个维度评估——原子一致性、域一致性、聚合一致性、证据忠实性。

Result: 10个SOTA LLM实验显示显著的误差累积效应：最优模型原子一致性达98.88%，端到端一致性却跌至45.13%，部分开源权重模型近乎0%；存在系统性证据-推理鸿沟，即使检索到高质量证据，仍有18.63–40.05%推理错误，盲猜率达48.28%。

Conclusion: 高结果准确率可能掩盖关键推理缺陷，临床部署亟需白盒逻辑验证。

Abstract: Evidence-based medicine demands strict logical consistency, yet current evaluations of large language models (LLMs) prioritize superficial label matching over genuine reasoning. We introduce LogiMed-RoB, a benchmark grounded in Cochrane Risk of Bias (RoB) 2.0 expert logic, comprising 860 randomized controlled trials (RCTs) and 14,820 queries. It evaluates models under the Hierarchical Logical Consistency (HLC) framework across four dimensions: Atomic Consistency, Domain Consistency, Aggregation Consistency, and Evidential Faithfulness. Experiments on 10 state-of-the-art LLMs reveal a catastrophic Error Compounding Effect: despite the top model reaching 98.88% Atomic Consistency, its end-to-end consistency collapses to 45.13%, with several open-weight architectures plummeting to nearly 0%. We further uncover a systematic evidence-reasoning gap: even when models retrieve high-quality evidence, they fail to deduce correct outcomes in 18.63-40.05% of cases, while Blind Guess Rates reach 48.28%. LogiMed-RoB demonstrates that high outcome accuracy can conceal critical reasoning flaws, underscoring the necessity of white-box logical verification for clinical deployment.

</details>


### [134] [An AI-Powered Culturally Aware Chatbot for Stress Detection and Wellness Support among Pakistani University Students Using NLP and Machine Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11199)
*Muhammad Fahad Bashir,Muhammad Afzal*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出并评估了一个面向巴基斯坦大学生的AI驱动、文化敏感的应激检测与心理健康支持系统，结合随机森林模型与开源大语言模型对话支持。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有数字心理健康工具主要面向西方情境开发，而巴基斯坦大学生面临学业、经济、家庭和人际关系等多重叠加压力，严重威胁其学业与心理发展，因此亟需文化适配的本地化解决方案。

Method: 采用随机森林机器学习模型，基于包含1100份问卷、20个心理/生理/学业/环境/社会特征的经验证学生应激数据集进行训练，划分三级应激严重程度；分类结果经OpenRouter API传入开源大语言模型，通过文化敏感的系统提示，以英语、乌尔都语和罗马乌尔都语开展心理健康对话；并通过特征重要性分析识别关键应激因素。

Result: 模型准确率达89.09%，宏F1分数为0.89；特征重要性分析显示，师生关系是该人群中第二重要的预测性应激因素，体现其文化意义。

Conclusion: 师生关系作为关键应激因素，凸显了开发区域敏感型心理健康系统的必要性；未来将使用经验证的DASS-21量表，从巴基斯坦多所高校不同学业阶段学生中采集原始数据，重点关注由FSc升入本科这一心理脆弱且研究不足的过渡阶段。

Abstract: With the existing digital mental health tools specifically developed for Western settings, Pakistani students are exposed to a uniquely compounded stress situation in their university that includes academic, financial, familial, and relational stressors, which have become a serious concern for academic and psychological development of students in Pakistani universities. This paper introduces a new, AI-driven and culturally sensitive stress detection and wellness support system that is tailored to the context of Pakistani university students. The system is based on a machine learning model called Random Forest which is trained using a validated student stress data set of 1100 responses on 20 features from psychological, physiological, academic, environmental and social aspects, with an accuracy of 89.09% and a macro F1-score of 0.89, in three stress severity levels. The classification outputs are passed on to an open-source large language model through OpenRouter API, where an appropriately crafted system prompt, culturally aware, gives the model a conversation about wellness, in English, Urdu and Roman Urdu. The second most predictive stress factor in this population identified by feature importance analysis was teacher-student relationship, which is a culturally important stress factor highlighting the need for region-aware mental health systems. Future research will involve primary data collection from students at various academic levels of Pakistani Universities with the validated DASS-21 instrument focusing on the students who are moving from FSc to undergraduate studies, which is a time of being psychologically vulnerable which is under-researched.

</details>


### [135] [CryptoL: Towards Scale Dominance and Physics Constraints Mitigation in Financial Multivariate Time Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11206)
*Yalda Taheri,Mohammad Hassan Heydari,Armon Rasooli,Maryam Amirshahkarami,Mohammad Ebrahim Mahdavi,Hossein Karshenas*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: CryptoL：一个统一的多变量加密货币OHLC预测框架，通过上下文归一化损失、通道相关归一化、尺度自适应数值稳定与软可行性损失，解决跨资产尺度异质性、非平稳性与OHLC结构约束问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 加密货币预测面临三个独特挑战：(1) 跨资产尺度极度异质；(2) 非平稳动态；(3) OHLC（开、高、低、收）变量间的结构性依赖。现有方法在RevIN反归一化时会将平方尺度权重引入MSE目标，导致大规模资产在共享模型优化中产生不成比例的影响，同时通道独立变换可能破坏K线顺序关系。

Method: CryptoL在RevIN流水线中以上下文归一化坐标评估预测误差，避免反归一化对MSE目标引入额外平方尺度加权，并通过经验风险与参数梯度几何形式化该效应；对比通道独立与通道相关归一化，采用共享通道相关仿射变换以保持K线顺序关系；引入尺度自适应数值稳定化以缓解固定归一化常数在多数量级资产上的失真；并加入软可行性损失惩罚违反OHLC不等式约束的预测。

Result: 在异质加密货币资产上的实验通过受控消融评估各组件，结果显示相对所比较的基线方法，在预测精度、训练稳定性以及财务有效OHLC预测频率方面均有提升。

Conclusion: CryptoL为尺度平衡优化、结构保持归一化、数值稳定化与约束感知的加密货币预测提供了一体化解决方案。

Abstract: Cryptocurrency forecasting presents a distinctive combination of extreme cross-asset scale heterogeneity, non-stationary dynamics, and structural dependencies among Open, High, Low, and Close (OHLC) variables. We present CryptoL, a unified framework designed to address these challenges within multivariate time-series forecasting. CryptoL evaluates forecasting error in context-normalized coordinates within the RevIN pipeline, preventing inverse normalization from introducing an additional squared-scale weighting into the MSE objective. We formally characterize this effect through the empirical risk and parameter-gradient geometry, establishing the conditions under which large-scale assets can disproportionately influence shared-model optimization. Beyond loss-space normalization, CryptoL examines channel-independent and channel-dependent normalization for OHLC data, showing that a shared channel-dependent affine transformation preserves candle-order relations that independent channel transformations need not preserve. The framework further incorporates scale-adaptive numerical stabilization to reduce distortions caused by a fixed normalization constant across assets spanning many orders of magnitude, together with a soft feasibility loss that penalizes violations of the defining OHLC inequalities. Experiments across heterogeneous cryptocurrency assets evaluate these components through controlled ablations and demonstrate improvements in forecasting accuracy, training stability, and the frequency of financially valid OHLC predictions relative to the considered baselines. CryptoL therefore provides an integrated approach to scale-balanced optimization, structure-preserving normalization, numerical stabilization, and constraint-aware cryptocurrency forecasting.

</details>


### [136] [A Voice-Interactive Multi-Agent System for Smart Operating Rooms: Architecture Design and Key Technologies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11231)
*Tianxiang Zhou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了SurgicalRoomAgent——一个基于大语言模型（LLM）的智能手术室语音交互多智能体系统，采用分层架构实现自然语言理解、设备控制、术中记录与手术报告生成，并研究了三项关键低延迟技术。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 手术室场景对系统实时性与多设备协同控制有严格要求，而LLM推理延迟、任务规划开销及有限上下文窗口是主要瓶颈，因此需要面向手术室场景设计低延迟、高信息密度的多智能体交互方案。

Method: 系统采用分层架构：语音交互流水线（唤醒、ASR、轮次检测、智能体推理、TTS）与智能体核心（技能注册表、任务规划器、设备管理器）。三项关键技术为：(1) KV Cache前缀预热，基于字节级最长公共前缀复用降低重复计算；(2) 流式部分JSON解析与早期并行任务执行；(3) 渐进式技能提示披露，依据用户角色、连接设备与手术阶段动态筛选系统提示。实现基于Qwen3-27B模型，推理引擎为llama.cpp/sglang。

Result: 实验分析表明，KV Cache前缀预热将重复计算开销从约500 ms降至数十毫秒；流式部分JSON解析与提前并行执行使端到端延迟降低约30%；系统在16,384 token上下文限制内可有效运行，多设备并行控制响应时间满足手术室实时性要求。

Conclusion: SurgicalRoomAgent验证了LLM多智能体系统在智能手术室中的可行性，所提出的KV Cache前缀预热、流式JSON解析与渐进式提示披露技术可有效降低延迟并在受限上下文下维持多设备实时并行控制。

Abstract: This paper presents SurgicalRoomAgent, a voice-interactive multi-agent system for smart operating rooms based on large language models (LLMs). The system achieves natural language understanding, device control, intraoperative recording, and surgical report generation through a layered architecture comprising a voice interaction pipeline (wake, ASR, turn detection, agent reasoning, TTS) and an agent core (skill registry, task planner, device manager). Three key technologies are investigated: (1) KV Cache prefix warming for low-latency inference, reducing recomputation overhead from approximately 500 ms to tens of milliseconds via byte-level Longest Common Prefix reuse; (2) streaming partial JSON parsing with early parallel task execution, reducing end-to-end latency by approximately 30%; and (3) progressive skill prompt disclosure, which dynamically filters system prompts based on user role, connected devices, and surgical phase to maximize information density within limited context windows. The system is implemented using the Qwen3-27B model with llama.cpp/sglang inference engines. Experimental analysis demonstrates effective operation within a 16,384-token context limit and multi-device parallel control response times meeting OR real-time requirements.

</details>


### [137] [NovGauge: A Fine-Grained Benchmark for Diagnosing LLMs' Capability in Paper Novelty Assessment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11234)
*Guoqiang Zhang,Kexin Tan,Ming Zhang,Li Ju,Wenqing Jing,Zhonghan Yue,Jiayi Chen,Shiqiang Wu,Shaofan Liu,Yue Zhang,Yuankai Ying,Yang Shi,Tao Gui,Qi Zhang,Xuanjing Huang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出NovGauge，一个以人类标注为锚的细粒度新颖性评估诊断基准，包含619个论文对和50个多论文集合，从任务、问题、方法三个维度独立标注，并设计级联诊断流程验证维度正确性、证据支撑与逻辑支撑；对18个LLM的评测揭示了0%-39%的幻觉率及超过70%的证据无法逻辑支撑结论的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
